# Paper workflow continuation — 8–9 November 2026

## Status and scope

This is the canonical continuation of the [first audit and build plan](AUDIT_AND_BUILD_PLAN.md), on the existing [Macro programme PR #10617](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/pull/10617). The Chairman requested further autonomous implementation, correction and enhancement across Paper. The work continues in the foreground; this saved milestone is not a completion claim or a request to resume.

Twelve additional files have received applied changes with native visual checks: Guide, Prophet, Market Memory, Policy Monitor, Market Ontology, Sector Intelligence, the Options W3 mobile companion, Risk Radar, Confluence Screener, Crypto Intelligence, T47 ranked search and BioCatalyst’s current builder navigation. The existing GMI and Terminal files also received deeper source-matched receipt and Company R3 amendments. Crypto now includes eight saved-case, historical-return and recovery states with an explicit storage/navigation build contract. The seven-file first pass remains accepted work for this mission and is not repeated here.

The root session is the only canvas writer among this session’s agents; independent live writers retain their own targets. Read-only design, international-language and architecture advisers contributed bounded judgments. Mutations use fresh snapshot guards and distinct operation identities beginning `paper-estate-cont-20261108-`. Initial changes used the Mastermind Paper connector; after its transport repeatedly timed out on reads, new operations continued through the authorized Studio Direct Paper owner. Completed operations were read back, never replayed; the detailed transport ruling below preserves that boundary. No runtime release, merge, hold change, message to another person, or live-model qualification has been performed.

#### Concrete source gaps added to the existing Build contract

The new paragraphs are 1EM-0, 1EQ-0 and 1EU-0 inside the existing Y1/YE sections of V1-0. They add missing source-specific details without repeating the existing record, recovery and comparison contract.

1. **Bind the full matrix.** The immutable source owner retains a derived newest-vintage object. Its `artifact_sha256` identifies that derived object; `provenance.upstream_artifact_sha256` and byte count bind the full vintage matrix containing the historical rows. Both row locators and original values must reopen in that exact matrix before the selected pair receives receipt/generation/cutoff claims.
2. **Use an explicit CPI rule.** `same_basis_level_change.v1` inherits `output_unit="bp"` and does not explicitly match `reference_start`/`reference_end`. The CPI adapter must use the incumbent comparison owner with explicit reference-period matching and `output_unit="index_level"`; do not silently reinterpret the frozen generic rule.
3. **Prove the actual risks.** A substituted matrix or unbound receipt cannot establish historical availability. A controlled, qualified same-period 100→101 fixture returns +1 index point. Different reference months or unknown index bases produce no numerical difference.

Primary sources: [newest-vintage projection and receipt owner](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/338a10852dcefa5d2bd07e5c6be0955c2b1e83fd/engine/neuralweb/market_memory_sources.py) and [comparison rule registry](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/338a10852dcefa5d2bd07e5c6be0955c2b1e83fd/engine/rates_command_basis.py). The Build addition was rendered and inspected. All nine exact working indicators were released.

#### Correct the measures, preserve the result

The displayed values and thresholds were preserved: oil +17.8%, breakeven +2 bp, real yield +65 bp, duration −0.59 pp; the path is still inactive with two of four conditions met. Later conditions cannot activate the unmet origin or establish causation.

“Sessions” was replaced with source-specific language:

- Oil and breakeven use 60 and 22 retained observations.
- QQQ and SPY use their respective 63-position histories; the dates need not align.
- Real yield is the existing owner's 63-period measure and may include carried-forward readings.
- MA50 is the 50-observation moving average's change over five positions, in raw units.
- The declared 5–60, 0–90 and 0–120 transition lags remain calendar days.

Primary source: [transmission owner](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/338a10852dcefa5d2bd07e5c6be0955c2b1e83fd/engine/transmission_chains.py), [rate owner](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/338a10852dcefa5d2bd07e5c6be0955c2b1e83fd/engine/rate_inflation_transmission.py), and [feature input calendar](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/338a10852dcefa5d2bd07e5c6be0955c2b1e83fd/engine/inputs.py).

The hidden older architecture note now acknowledges the implemented [Scenario](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/338a10852dcefa5d2bd07e5c6be0955c2b1e83fd/engine/ontology_scenario.py) and [Portfolio membership](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/338a10852dcefa5d2bd07e5c6be0955c2b1e83fd/engine/ontology_portfolio.py) readers. The useful constraint is no additional independent canonical owner or duplicate lifecycle; existing typed request/projection shapes are not forbidden. Dated membership still does not establish causal exposure or P&L.

The new Build cards AUL/AUP/AUT specify exact state mapping and return, source/permission matching, no automatic resend, and a restrained 180 ms detail reveal with instant reduced-motion behavior. Values, probabilities and unmet causal links must not animate into misleading states. This is a motion implementation contract, not a claim that runtime animation was executed.

Native main, source, answer, desktop source and complete Build views were inspected. All eight exact working indicators were released.

## 6. Sector Intelligence — qualified timing and a focused comparison

[Current comparison](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6FAXA774QT1VRDMAG7DFQ2W/p-4-0) · [Expanded context](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6FAXA774QT1VRDMAG7DFQ2W/p-8-0) · [Cycles variants](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6FAXA774QT1VRDMAG7DFQ2W/p-2-0) · [Unbound originals](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6FAXA774QT1VRDMAG7DFQ2W/p-7-0) · [Build](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6FAXA774QT1VRDMAG7DFQ2W/p-5-0)

### Put the comparison actions and evidence first

FHS-0 and FTX-0 now describe the observed cycle phase without presenting a future timing range as “not a forecast.” The original desktop FKT-0 and mobile FXB-0 dossier actions moved next to group/window selection, in FEQ-0 and FQO-0. Their label is “Inspect these sectors.”

The two-sector comparison no longer requires reading the full market map and a second whole-market movement list. A collapsed “Whole-market context” control keeps the selected pair’s falling-rank conclusion, five-session scope, 11/11 coverage and 30 October 2026 observation date visible. Its expanded design retains the complete original context:

| State | Desktop | Mobile |
|---|---|---|
| Default disclosure | HA7-0 in FB7-0 | HAG-0 in FO0-0 |
| Expanded context | H9R-0 | H9Z-0 |
| Original retained content | FL1-0 | FXN-0 and G1I-0 |

The full comparison table stays in the default view: price measures, source-population differences, phase, unavailable current model, historical priors and unavailable cells retain their meaning. Tag definitions and Methodology/Coverage/Snapshot record remain outside the disclosure. An orphan, unlabeled mobile legend ring G1C-0 moved into Build reference HB1-0; its meaning was not invented.

Build HAP-0 specifies native details/summary behavior, a minimum 44 px target, keyboard operation, exclusion of closed links from the tab sequence, and return with the same pair/window/benchmark/snapshot, expanded state and originating row. Five-session rank history must not inherit the 21/63/126-session return selection. Missing rank observations produce incomplete history, not zero or a falling-rank conclusion. Motion is limited to 160 ms opacity and ≤4 px translation, with instant reduced-motion behavior; numeric meaning never animates.

### Correct all eight timing variants together

The specific Nov 2026 / Feb 2027 / Jun 2027 timing fixture was not bound to a precise publication, confirmed-turn anchor and historical cohort in the inspected design. A generic batch date, phase age or current-leg caption does not establish that binding.

Across all eight EN/ZH, theme and device variants, the hero now leads with observed phase and age. Historical timing uses a compact unavailable state with an unverified-source explanation. The unbound future shading, boundary/median lines and future-window label were removed from the main price chart, while both historical series, confirmed-turn markers, axes, current-date marker, controls and values were preserved.

The original vector chart, timing strip, date footnote and superseded qualifier were moved by ID into a clearly marked reference page. No original artwork or data was deleted.

This is a design qualification state, not a finding that every possible live Sector publication is invalid. A separately qualified historical rhythm can be shown once its own evidence is established, even when a current hazard-model estimate remains unavailable.

### Build from the actual source contract

Build H8Z-0 contains the source binding, calculation, cohort boundary, elapsed states and real-consumer proof requirements:

1. Use the incumbent [SectorCyclesEvidence receipt](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/9fc460ff36345387c3a2c5435b079852e8488da0/templates/sector_cycles.js), requiring the active matching publication and relevant verified source part. Bind the exact subject, structure basis, confirmed turn and ordered historical cohort. Publication integrity alone does not expose missing calculation inputs.
2. [project_next](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/9fc460ff36345387c3a2c5435b079852e8488da0/engine/cycle_ontology.py) requires at least three confirmed turns and adds P25/median/P75 half-cycle spacing to the last confirmed turn. The next turn kind is opposite that anchor. These are typical historical quartiles, not earliest/latest bounds or a calibrated probability interval.
3. The [producer](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/9fc460ff36345387c3a2c5435b079852e8488da0/engine/sector_cycles.py) computes `proj` from all swings while stamping `rhythm` from filtered exported turns. Do not mix projection dates with rhythm status, counts or anchor without proving cohort/window parity. Extend the existing producer/disclosure contract when necessary; do not create a frontend projection owner.
4. `period_yrs` values are full-cycle equivalents: twice the half-cycle durations. The native forecast card’s half-cycle label must not be copied onto these doubled values.
5. [Rhythm states](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/9fc460ff36345387c3a2c5435b079852e8488da0/engine/cycle_rhythm.py) remain anchored as the evaluation date passes the median and upper quartile. Missing inputs, insufficient history, source replacement and cohort mismatch yield truthful unavailable/changed-source outcomes.
6. Prove one exact implementation head, publication, selected subject and anchor/cohort tuple in the real existing consumer. Reproduce the months; check provisional exclusion, insufficient history, mismatch, fixed-window elapsed states, source replacement and return. Synthetic fixtures establish behavior only. [Dossier](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/74930f6931773b23072f91590319dc39fe5a7d07/templates/sector_dossier_cycle.js) continues opening the existing full Cycles evidence destination.

The held p-3-0 work, BML acceptance, G4P review-only canary, GPL return proposal and PR10703/10707 release holds were preserved. These design repairs do not qualify a current model or admit a release.

## 7. Options Watch/review — explicit firing selection and recovery

[Review amendment](https://app.paper.design/file/01M5FNCKAND6CBSJNYM5FZWR8Y/p-2-0) · [Build contract](https://app.paper.design/file/01M5FNCKAND6CBSJNYM5FZWR8Y/p-3-0) · [Accepted original](https://app.paper.design/file/01M5FNCKAND6CBSJNYM5FZWR8Y/p-1-0)

The accepted main W3 file remains read-only. All writes in the mobile companion were scoped to new p-2-0 and p-3-0; the accepted p-1-0 boards were not edited by this session. A concurrent writer’s original updates were observed during readback and preserved. This is not a claim that the original file stayed byte-identical.

The new amendment starts with the saved question and **Draft assessment: Inconclusive**, because “rotation fade” has no defined condition in this fixture. The watch, selected firing and assessment are separate concepts. A compact **View alert receipt** entry replaces the full receipt block on the primary review path; the full receipt has its own return and an explicit **Review without a firing** action. That second path saves an investigation assessment with the optional firing link omitted, not a new kind of watch-specific assessment.

| Design state | Native root | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Review a recorded firing | 3A-0, candidate 3D-0 | Question, watch, draft conclusion, compact receipt entry, explicit binding immediately before Save assessment |
| Receipt detail | 69-0 | Recorded condition, firing time, source clocks, delivery and identifier; return with the same draft |
| Review without a firing | 77-0, assessment 7B-0 | Investigation assessment without the optional firing identifier |
| Watch status unavailable | 86-0 | Saved condition retained; Retry status and View revision 3, without claiming not armed |
| Save result unavailable | 8M-0 | Draft retained; Check save result observes the original operation |
| Saved, reopen unavailable | 8Y-0 | Committed revision identified separately; Open revision 4 retries readback |
| Implementation contract | 65-0 | Existing owners, source semantics, identity, state transitions, motion and proof |

The page header 36-0 marks these as design candidates with illustrative data, tied to the current source pin. The pages were arranged after the original, without switching the user’s active page.

### Correct the source and action meaning

The recorded condition is **price > 230.00**, matching the strict `above` evaluator. The observed value remains 230.12. Re-arm retains the fixed threshold; a changed wall level does not silently update that threshold. The explanation reflects the incumbent five-minute check and hub-quote/EOD-close behavior, rather than claiming a fresh quote on every firing.

The source clock is unavailable unless the specific receipt supplies it. Firing time is not relabeled as quote time. The current quote is **Not loaded** rather than an inferred historical value. Receipt delivery is **Unconfirmed** unless matching delivery evidence proves a stronger status. Neither an absent receipt identifier nor an unreadable queue establishes “not delivered.”

The draft note now says the recorded price was 230.12 and the timing question remains inconclusive because rotation fade is undefined. It does not assert that the price event occurred two sessions after an unverified rotation event.

The binding panel immediately above **Save assessment** names the selected watch, firing identifier/time and base investigation revision. The builder contract freezes that selection while editing; a newly arriving firing cannot silently replace it. An unlinked investigation review omits the optional firing identifier and retains the same existing investigation save owner.

### Current code findings and implementation sequence

All source links in this section use Terminal `cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3`.

1. **Fix status presentation through the current watch owner.** [investigationWatch.ts](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/lib/investigationWatch.ts) distinguishes unknown from armed/fired. [InvestigationWatchPanel.tsx](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/workspaces/InvestigationWatchPanel.tsx) currently maps the remaining branch to NOT ARMED. Preserve the unknown state in presentation and let Retry status use the existing reader.

2. **Bind assessment to the user’s chosen firing.** [InvestigationReviewPanel.tsx](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/workspaces/InvestigationReviewPanel.tsx) currently submits `review.fired?.fire_event_id`; [investigationReview.ts](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/lib/investigationReview.ts) projects the latest firing across linked alerts. Add explicit local selection in the existing workspace/panel, preserving it with the draft and base revision. Do not create another alert or assessment persistence owner.

3. **Keep per-firing evidence separate from current state.** The review owner reads a receipt’s own payload value. [alertsView.ts](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/lib/alertsView.ts) chooses latest delivery by alert in its default path. An older selected firing needs its matching delivery row; do not borrow the newest alert delivery. Retain read/unavailable/absent queue distinctions.

4. **Expose what the evaluator actually records.** [ingest/alerts_engine.py](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/ingest/alerts_engine.py) checks strict `last > value` / `last < value`; its ordinary price source can fall back from hub quote to EOD close. The wall condition establishes an initial inside/outside observation and fires on an outside-to-inside transition. The ordinary firing payload does not populate the review reader’s optional `source_asof` and `corrects` fields. Do not fabricate those values or impose a universal stale-price pause absent from this owner.

5. **Use the existing save protocol and separate commit from reopening.** [investigationSave.ts](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/lib/investigationSave.ts) retains principal, original operation ID and expected revision through pending/uncertain outcomes. [InvestigationWorkspace.tsx](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/workspaces/InvestigationWorkspace.tsx) already checks the original operation outcome and opens the exact returned revision. Validation/conflict retains the draft. Uncertain save performs the existing outcome read, not a new write. A known committed revision with failed readback remains saved.

The reopening route remains `/analysis?view=investigations&investigation=<id>&revision=<n>`, with existing access and manifest validation. The [W3 freeze](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/docs/OPTIONS_R1_W3_FREEZE.md) and [Options programme](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/docs/OPTIONS_R1_PROGRAM.md) remain the acceptance context. W4C predictive admission and missing producer fields are separate held work; this amendment does not admit them.

### Visual checks and required functional proof

Native screenshots verified the main 390 px review, receipt, unlinked assessment, unavailable status, uncertain save, saved/readback failure and full Build contract. The final primary view shows the draft answer before the watch card and keeps the selected firing/revision immediately above Save. The receipt retains separate source and delivery fields and a clear return. All eight exact owned root working indicators were released; the independently edited original was not included.

Build specifies ≥44 px actions, focus returning to the actual opener, retained draft and selected identity, one outcome announcement, ≤160 ms opacity/≤4 px translation, and instant reduced motion. Required implementation proof covers strict threshold equality, already-beyond arming, wall first observation, missing quotes, malformed watch state, old/new firing selection, unlinked review, unreadable versus absent delivery, original-operation uncertain recovery, conflicts, committed/readback failure, and current route access. EN/ZH, theme and 390/820/1440 px runtime checks remain acceptance work, not tests claimed by this native design pass.

## 8. Options Flow → expiry context — a source-matched mobile journey

[Native amendment](https://app.paper.design/file/01M5FNCKAND6CBSJNYM5FZWR8Y/p-4-0) · [Implementation contract](https://app.paper.design/file/01M5FNCKAND6CBSJNYM5FZWR8Y/p-5-0)

The accepted ET-0 and KT-0 boards in the full main file were inspected without modification. They remain useful design references. The dated W3 field-policy amendment and the current Terminal consumers establish a narrower, buildable default: contract-level activity, named-method side estimates, explicit missing evidence, and an expiry’s published ATM context.

### Primary path and detail states

| State | Native root | Result |
|---|---|---|
| Contract activity | AH-0 | Exact AAPL 230 Call / 23 Oct identity; 6,000 reported contracts, premium and average price; correctly scoped size/OI ratio; one primary Compare expiry IV action |
| Expiry comparison | AU-0 | Published ATM IV, two useful differences, three aligned comparison bars, percent-only expected move and supplied earnings context |
| Contract evidence | B7-0 | Record meaning, arithmetic, unavailable fields and source timing, with return to the actual contract |
| Volatility details | BK-0 | Full curve, supplied/interpolated basis, explicit reference-strike ratios, calculation and separate published band |
| Retained Flow after failed refresh | JG-0 | Earlier reading identified, Retry refresh reuses the existing owner |
| Feed absent | JK-0 | Proven absence distinguished from unreadability |
| Loaded feed with no filter matches | JO-0 | Filter scope retained; Clear filters changes filter state |
| Exact expiry has no ATM IV | JS-0 | Other expiries remain available; explicit choice and return |
| Volatility snapshot read failed | KL-0 | AAPL and the requested expiry retained during retry |
| Later view date, past expiry | KX-0 | Historical reading retained; no future expected move |
| Incomplete contract record | L9-0 | Unknown side, missing OI and unavailable ratio remain explicit; supported navigation stays available |

Header AC-0 identifies the fixture and source pin. Build LL-0 maps every state, current owner, required adapter and acceptance case. New pages are ordered after the existing Watch/review and Build pages. All writes remain in the companion’s new pages; this phase did not touch its independently edited original page.

The Flow first screen puts the observation and next action ahead of full evidence. The Volatility first screen preserves the correct 36.9% supplied value, +2.1 vol-point difference versus 7D and +7.5 versus 30D. Three bars share a declared 0–40% scale. Full curve, skew and calculations are available through a named detail entry rather than crowding the primary path.

### Contract activity, ratio and missing evidence

The [projector](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/lib/flowPrintInspector.ts) treats the wire as contract-level activity, potentially combining prints. It preserves the owner premium and compares it with size × average price ×100. Receipt time, conditions, lineage, quote context and confidence are null; packages remain unadmitted. The new detail view states those absences rather than simulating exchange precision, related legs or correction/cancellation evidence.

The ratio uses the **selected record’s size**, not an unrelated whole-session volume. For the explicit fixture, 6,000/32,000=0.1875, displayed **0.19×**. The [current InspectorPane mount](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/flowdesk/InspectorPane.tsx) passes no `oiAsOf` to [PrintInspector](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/flowdesk/PrintInspector.tsx), whose default is null. Accordingly, both the primary and detail view now say **OI date unavailable / Not supplied**. A prior-session date is not inferred.

The same InspectorPane has an older `sideLean` fallback that maps unhandled values to sell, while the projector supports unknown. The Build contract calls for one unknown-safe presentation across both surfaces. It preserves a valid partial record and its supported actions; missing OI does not become zero or block unrelated navigation.

### Transport and source status

[flowStream.ts](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/lib/flowStream.ts) explicitly separates connection from read status and producer freshness. A connected SSE transport can report absent or unavailable; failed reads can retain earlier data. Retry uses the existing same-key reader. The amendment gives each of retained failure, proven absence and filter-only emptiness a distinct title and action.

[flowFreshness.ts](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/lib/flowFreshness.ts) validates measured `live_flow.meta/v2` timing and accepts only `source_asof` for derived-artifact source age. Build time is not a substitute. Independently arriving metadata must not make an older selected record look refreshed, and source-response age is not exchange latency.

[FlowFreshnessReceipt](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/flowdesk/FlowFreshnessReceipt.tsx) currently renders Market closed for `last_session`, although the classifier can produce that state from an old or missing payload date. The builder correction is to distinguish the payload’s dated status from independently established exchange-open status. No new timing authority or refresh lifecycle is proposed.

### The navigation adapter is required, not already wired

[FlowDeskView](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/flowdesk/FlowDeskView.tsx) owns local selected-event and filter state. [OptionsHubView](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/OptionsHubView.tsx) currently mounts FlowDeskView and VolView without a root/expiry handoff. [VolView](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/vol/VolView.tsx) initializes its own root and expiry choices. Merely changing tabs would not fulfill the AAPL/23 Oct promise.

Build LL-0 specifies a bounded extension of the incumbent hub/child callbacks:

1. Emit selected event identity and root/expiry/strike/right from Flow.
2. Retain the origin in the existing hub and use its existing tab owner.
3. Apply the requested root through VolView’s existing reset/read lifecycle; select the exact expiry only after that root’s inventory arrives.
4. Show the missing-expiry state if that expiry is unavailable. Do not silently display SPY or a different expiry beneath the original breadcrumb.
5. Return through the same owner to the actual origin control, with a proven same-instance selection/filter/scroll restoration.

[OptionsWorkspace](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/workspaces/OptionsWorkspace.tsx) remains the URL writer. `tab=volatility` is the Volatility destination; `tab=vol` is Screener. Its `replaceState` behavior does not supply a browser-Back restoration contract. Existing keep-alive mounts are useful, but they do not freeze a changing feed or prove full-reload recovery. No additional feed history or snapshot store is introduced.

The [Largest/0DTE board](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/options/OptionsFlowBoardView.tsx) emits contract context while its hub mount accepts only root and opens Tickers. Until a real contract handoff preserves that identity, its truthful action is **Open ticker activity**. This is recorded as a separate bounded adapter issue, not silently presented as a working contract permalink.

### Volatility math and clocks remain explicit

The correct existing formulas and fixtures were preserved:

- Total-variance interpolation from 14D at 32.2% and 30D at 29.4% gives **30.480157…% at 21D**, displayed 30.5%.
- The current 105% skew row at strike 248 is supplied under the incumbent less-than-0.5 percentage-point tolerance around reference 236.5. It is not replaced with a different historical test fixture. The table now explicitly says **% of reference strike**, with **100%=236.5**.
- Unsupported 90%/110% wings stay blank, and the delta axis remains unsupported.
- The IV approximation is **36.9%×√(2/252)=3.287313…%**, displayed ±3.3%, without a confidence level.

These behaviors come from [volTenorComparison.ts](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/lib/volTenorComparison.ts). [VolComparePanel](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/vol/VolComparePanel.tsx) uses source-date calendar days for constant tenors, and viewer-ET-date weekdays for the expected-move horizon. Exchange holidays are not subtracted. The native illustration explicitly uses view date 21 Oct 2026; the later-view state uses 9 Nov and withholds a future move for the past 23 Oct expiry.

The interpolated 11 Nov date is labeled as a constant-tenor date, not a supplied exchange expiry. The newer AC40 behavior remains the required implementation: absent source date yields no synthetic date; a synthetic date on/before the view date is qualified as coming from the earlier source read.

The ordinary hub mount supplies no `liveSpot`, so the new default is **percent only**. A price range requires the existing qualified stamped-spot input. The current event join supplies at most root earnings from `fund.json`; it does not supply the accepted concept’s FOMC row, date-change history or announcement time. Calendar-window inclusion does not establish pre-settlement timing.

The separate published one-day band retains its own multiplier, horizon, date and unstated method. It is not fused with the IV approximation.

### Additional implementation risks and verification

The current comparison should reuse [volShared](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/vol/volShared.tsx) duplicate-identity admission consistently across table, anchors and skew; passing raw arrays to helpers must not re-admit conflicting identities. Missing IV for the selected expiry must not imply no usable expiry globally. The existing fund/moves readers also need to preserve failed versus absent outcomes before a UI promises those distinctions.

Native checks covered all eleven screen/state roots and the full Build sheet. An initial text-contrast import issue was repaired in place; final JSX readback across all screen/state roots found no remaining black text on the dark surfaces. Two final focused screenshots verified the corrected OI-date qualification. Independent arithmetic checks reproduced the displayed ratio, IV differences, 21D interpolation and expected move. All thirteen exact owned root working indicators were released.

The Build contract specifies 160 ms opacity/≤4px translation, instant reduced motion, ≥44px actions, actual-opener focus restoration and one outcome announcement. Required runtime proof covers exact selection return, changing feeds, wrong-root responses, missing expiry, unknown side, zero/missing OI, mismatched arithmetic, retained failed reads, absence versus filtering, source/view clocks, holiday horizons, duplicate identities and optional-input outcomes at 390/820/1440 px in EN/ZH and both themes. These are future implementation gates; no runtime wiring, browser execution or release acceptance is claimed.

## 9. Risk Radar: qualification before retained probability

**Native file:** [Market Dashboard Popups / Risk Radar](https://app.paper.design/file/01M38EVF74SJWG1GANN5VP380Z/p-1-0). This is a hierarchy correction to the existing main/evidence journey, not a new model, data source or inspector.

### Applied design changes

| Variant | Main | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Desktop dark EN | `1-0` | `8PS-0` |
| Mobile dark EN | `8R6-0` | `8SH-0` |
| Mobile light EN | `97F-0` | `98K-0` |
| Mobile light ZH | `8YA-0` | `8ZF-0` |
| Mobile dark ZH | `99D-0` | `9AI-0` |

The four mobile main views now show a compact **Drop-model evidence / Calibration unverified** entry (ZH: 下跌模型证据 / 校准尚未核实), instead of making the unverified 13% the row's visual headline. It targets the existing model section in evidence. The entire row should be one accessible action, named with its status; the arrow is decorative. The general evidence, seven-driver and leadership routes remain intact.

All five evidence cards lead with a retained-model label and **Calibration unverified**, followed by the unchanged 13% versus retained normal reference of 18% and the existing event definition. On desktop main, `8TE-0` was moved ahead of the probability table, keeping its ID; 13% now uses neutral text so the qualification owns the warning color.

The retained desktop table remains **5D 2%, 10D 6%, 21D 13%, normal reference 18%, model/base-rate 0.73×**. The 36/100 risk index, 57.5 driver, 94/48 gauges, all seven drivers, leadership, historical evidence and return controls were preserved. No source value was recalculated or promoted to current/live status.

### Verified source fit

The source was read directly at Macro `338a10852dcefa5d2bd07e5c6be0955c2b1e83fd`:

- [engine/risk_radar.py](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/338a10852dcefa5d2bd07e5c6be0955c2b1e83fd/engine/risk_radar.py), L228–240 and L1306–1347: calm/watch probabilities are 0.02/0.06/0.13; the unrounded 21-observation baseline is **0.178**. The model lift is `round(0.13 / 0.178, 2) = 0.73`. Never recompute it from the rounded displayed 13/18, which would produce 0.72. Keep displayed-model lift distinct from state-only lift, which excludes the conjunction adjustment.
- The same owner, L367–425, matches the complete effective probability surface and conjunction bumps, then the exact horizon/probability cell. Evidence is display provenance, not another confidence rule or model input.
- [engine/risk_radar_audit.py](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/338a10852dcefa5d2bd07e5c6be0955c2b1e83fd/engine/risk_radar_audit.py), L269–284, uses the first closing observation strictly after as-of, the prior available close as base, and the minimum of the next H closes for the drawdown event.

This source consistency does **not** bind the retained 23 September snapshot to an accepted calibration cell. The separate **300-observation / observed 1%** history remains unbound and must not be presented as validation of the 13% model. “Unverified” qualifies that missing match; it does not declare the model invalid.

### Builder and verification contract

The existing pickup map `10V-0` was amended at `199-0`, `19C-0`, `19D-0` with the model-section target, retained-value requirements, unrounded ratio, calibration binding and return rules. Preserve US, 21D, opened snapshot, scrolling and invoking focus. Back/Escape closes detail. Source-detail retry remains conditional on an existing permitted read owner for that same snapshot; otherwise preserve available values and Back with an unavailable state. New data requires explicit review before replacing the opened snapshot.

Native screenshots reviewed all ten affected product variants and the pickup map. Light-mode status text uses the existing `--mx-light-ink-warn` token: calculated contrast is **5.95:1** on the light background and **6.32:1** on white; dark status is **7.59:1** on the dark panel. Final JSX readback found **no added or lost node IDs** across the eleven roots and only the 27 intended text/style changes plus the one ordering change. Existing return controls, event definitions, references, drivers and historical values were unchanged. All eleven exact working indicators were released.

The newer Follow-save and generic popup interaction/future-state work on `p-9-0` and `p-8-0`, Pullback studies, other popup redesigns and historical references were preserved. Native visual QA is not browser proof: 320px, 200% text, keyboard, focus, actual disclosure/return and source-read behavior remain implementation checks.

- [templates/_gmi_product_macros.html.j2](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/80d6ff71b9913524c4a381b543ec7a53383f50dc/templates/_gmi_product_macros.html.j2), L23 and L39: separate native card receipt and topic-context disclosures.

- [templates/gmi.js](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/80d6ff71b9913524c4a381b543ec7a53383f50dc/templates/gmi.js), L199–243, L258–273, L292–317: qualified return, optional receipt fields, dynamic disclosure, card replacement and locale-triggered rebuild.

This newer candidate is narrower than the broader internal model at the retained Macro pin. **26 February 2024 is the source publication date only.** The valid knowledge date is projected separately as `theme_links.first_seen`; retrieval clocks, curation review dates and magnitude states are not in the connection-card payload. Do not import those fields from the internal model, infer live delivery/revenue exposure, or expand the exact public observation allowance.

### Concrete implementation amendment

1. Keep the two existing native disclosures. Apply Theme details copy to the topic summary, and keep card receipt labels/fields consistent between the Jinja macro and the JS connection renderer. The selected receipt is already in the rendered card; opening it requires no new request, store, inspector or route.
2. In the existing locale branch of `gmi.js`, capture transient disclosure state before `sync()` replaces card children. Match only a present, unique existing public record within the selected theme and the same source key/hash. Restore only still-supplied matching cards, then discard the capture.
3. Preserve focus on an intact language control. Only restore a corresponding summary/action if its previous focused node was replaced. If the card disappears, use the current topic heading or available empty-state action. Never restore stale DOM or source URLs.
4. Missing/duplicate identities must not fall back to row order, ticker, company name or translated text. `evidence=1` remains the evidence-only topic filter; it must not become the disclosure-open bit. No receipt, source hash or internal ledger identity is added to `gmi_return`.
5. Retain the existing CSS wrapping and desktop/mobile row patterns. Increase the current summary minimum from 40px to the shown 44px. Keep expansion immediate; any optional chevron/opacity motion is ≤120ms and absent with reduced motion.
6. Verify static/no-JS/dynamic parity, two open records across locale changes, reorder/missing/duplicate identities, removed cards, focus ownership and 200% text. A missing field can receive Not recorded copy only on a still-admitted card. The current exact Micron predicate otherwise withholds the card; template support for an absent URL does not prove that it is a reachable ordinary source outcome.

The existing return owner admits only qualified theme/company-catalogue presentation context, a bounded query and at most one upstream theme return. It does not prove immutable source snapshot, exact opener, scroll or historical-stack restoration. A removed issuer file needs its existing serving/unavailable path verified; the issuer template's empty-card branch does not establish that response. No synthetic Back or replacement-company fallback was introduced.

### Native verification and preservation

Final JSX comparisons across the eight current theme boards found **no lost existing IDs**, exactly one intended existing label change per board, and exactly one new two-node receipt entry per board. Every public identifier appears exactly once in each expanded state, with original bytes intact. Native screenshots checked both full 390px workflows, the 320px component, the final desktop card, the full existing desktop page, themed entries and the seven-module Build sheet. The long hash wraps without clipping; desktop labels align; all inspected text uses the established theme tokens.

The entire S05 root `73X-0` was read back **byte-identical** to its preimage. The ten first-pass historical badges, both company-catalogue boards, original S03–S05 guidance and historical references were preserved. Fourteen exact affected roots were released. PR #10676's Draft/release holds and protected model-run boundaries were unchanged; no fenced result was consumed and no runtime source, model, CI, release or deployment action was performed.

These are applied Paper states and a source-matched implementation plan. The locale-state repair and 44px CSS amendment are not claimed as implemented runtime behavior.

**Existing file:** Terminal, [Company · Production Suite · R3](https://app.paper.design/file/01M3NSZGE8JWN2EZCSF0DT05RM/p-S-0).

The known empty 390px successor was re-read with zero children before assembly. It now reuses existing historical Study B content, native status bar, family/view control, metric and comparator controls, source action and separate next-quarter link. The new 320px and Chinese states reuse the established content and language patterns. Duplicate headings and study annotations remain in their source studies rather than being copied into the primary mobile workflow.

| Root | Applied state | Key actions |
|---|---|---|
| `1D9K-0` | 390px EN dark, formerly empty R3 successor | Source `28EC-0`; next quarter `28FP-0` |
| `28O8-0` | 320px EN dark, full phone composition | Source `28PF-0`; next quarter `28PH-0` |
| `28PK-0` | 390px ZH light | Source `28R1-0`; next quarter `28R3-0` |
| `28PX-0` | Proposed incompatible Q2/Q3 retained selection | Explicit repair `28RP-0`; publisher source `28RJ-0` |
| `28RS-0` | Eight-module Build amendment below the compositions | Custody, navigation, typed pair, calculation, return, recovery, interaction proof and exact source pins |

The main reading order is company and historical date → family/view → metric/period and comparator → reported value → prior guidance → qualified difference → source → separate next-quarter outlook. The main 390px screen fits its original 844px frame. The narrow composition wraps without clipping; the Chinese version keeps the original fiscal-period wording, explicit USD units and established light-theme positive ink. Product screens carry only the information needed to understand the comparison. Owner contracts and implementation limits sit in the Build board.

### Historical fixture and arithmetic

Root re-read the official NVIDIA [Q1 FY2026 release dated 28 May 2025](https://investor.nvidia.com/news/press-release-details/2025/NVIDIA-Announces-Financial-Results-for-First-Quarter-Fiscal-2026/) and [Q2 FY2026 release dated 27 August 2025](https://investor.nvidia.com/news/press-release-details/2025/NVIDIA-Announces-Financial-Results-for-Second-Quarter-Fiscal-2026/default.aspx).

Q2 actual is 46.743 USD bn for the quarter ended 27 July 2025. Prior Q2 guidance is 45.000 USD bn, issued 28 May; its ±2% range is 44.10–45.90. The unrounded calculation is `(46.743 − 45.000) / 45.000 × 100 = 3.873333…%`, displayed as **+3.9% above prior guidance**, with the midpoint basis explicitly stated. Analyst consensus remains unavailable.

The separate Q3 outlook is 54.0 USD bn ±2%, issued 27 August; its derived range is 52.92–55.08. That outlook is not the Q2 comparator. Public access to these releases does not establish a retained historical copy, acquisition receipt, replay eligibility or an as-known-then result. The existing evidence sheets retain their unavailable-copy limitation.

### Current architecture and concrete implementation gaps

Root read the following at Terminal **`cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3`**:

- [finPageFamilies.ts](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/fin/finPageFamilies.ts): the seven-family map gives Lab only the existing `lab` page.
- [ResearchWorkspaceNav.tsx](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/fin/ResearchWorkspaceNav.tsx): family selection dispatches its current default through the existing navigation owner.
- [MegaPane.tsx](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/components/fin/MegaPane.tsx): `page=lab` mounts `TechLabPanel`; it is not an implemented Expectations view.
- [earningsExpectationBoundary.ts](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/blob/cfe14c49f2e0166c0ded5e6f23a274bb663d0fd3/terminal/lib/earningsExpectationBoundary.ts): the incumbent boundary admits byte-replayed reported evidence and formatted percentage guidance. Its current regex already permits signed percentages; the earlier D07 “nonnegative only” description is stale. The relevant gap is the absence of this typed dollar-guidance pair, not a missing sign in a regex.
- The inspected `AnalysisWorkspace.tsx` and `analysisRoute.ts` retain company/page identity, validated URL and Back behavior, and company-change clearing of investigation pins. None of those pins proves restoration of the two proposed comparison inputs.

At D07 **`a4bb0c93f0ef47a29687496ea52349195a8f08ae`**, [SEMANTIC_TASKS.md S12](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/a4bb0c93f0ef47a29687496ea52349195a8f08ae/research/design_candidates/production_design_20261102/d07/SEMANTIC_TASKS.md) still leaves the historical comparator `OWNER_BINDING_REQUIRED`. The [D07 README](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/a4bb0c93f0ef47a29687496ea52349195a8f08ae/research/design_candidates/production_design_20261102/d07/README.md) preserves the foundation and final-composition qualification boundary.

The Build amendment specifies these narrow extensions:

1. Extend the existing Lab navigation and content owners deliberately, retaining Technical Lab access. Reuse the seven-family mobile chooser at `p-9-1 / 5ML-0`, sheet `5Q1-0`. Do not invent an already-supported `page=expectations`, duplicate company store or independent route vocabulary.
2. Require the existing filing/estimates/Earnings owners to supply a typed pair with the same issuer/listing, target fiscal period and period end, metric, consolidation/reporting basis, currency and numeric unit. Keep both source identities/versions and each input’s publication, acquisition and retained-copy state.
3. Distinguish the **release quarter** from the **guidance target quarter**: the Q1 publication contains Q2 guidance. Equal duration or a prior publication date alone cannot establish compatibility. The Financials year-over-year selector is not the guidance selector.
4. For this revenue comparison, require a finite positive midpoint and compute from unrounded values. Missing or zero does not become a denominator. Do not parse formatted strings such as `45.000 USD bn` to establish machine identity, basis or source qualification.
5. Re-qualify after company, period, metric, basis or source changes. Current event/evidence URL pins do not establish a source-bound comparison pair. No analyst beat, price reaction, watch, alert or investigation action is implied.

### Source and return behavior

EN main and narrow source actions reuse `22CM-0`; the ZH action reuses `22SU-0`. Each actual invoker must be captured independently. Close restores it only while the listing, fiscal target, metric, basis and both source versions still match. Lost context uses the incumbent owner’s labeled recovery state; it must not restore stale focus or select another period silently.

Next-quarter actions lead separately to the existing Q3 outlook states `1NIJ-0` / `1NPH-0`. They do not alter the Q2 comparison. The 320px source/next-quarter controls are `28PF-0` / `28PH-0`, not the original 390px nodes.

### Incompatible-quarter recovery

New `28PX-0` is a proposed invalid retained-state branch, not an additional eligible option in the normal Q2 comparator picker. It keeps **Q2 actual 46.743** and **Q3 guidance 54.0** visible with their publication information, and says **Comparison unavailable — Q2 actual and Q3 guidance cover different quarters.** It contains no percentage or derived comparison chart.

**Use prior Q2 guidance** explicitly resolves the existing owner’s Q2 guidance object, qualifies it, and only then replaces the comparator. Failure or denied access keeps the incompatible selection with its reason; the actual period and value remain fixed. The mismatch source action opens the 27 August publisher release directly. It must not open the valid-Q2 calculation sheet as if that calculation belonged to the Q2/Q3 pair. That calculation becomes appropriate again after successful repair.

### Native proof, preservation and remaining gates

Final JSX readback confirmed that Study B `1NII-0`, Study G `1NPG-0`, R3 Expectations desktop `1CD3-0`, R3 brief `1BEK-0` and EN evidence sheet `22CM-0` were **byte-identical** to their preimages. Original D07 Build `1NFS-0` changed only in text node `1NGO-0`: it now points to the provisional assembly and `28RS-0` instead of describing the successor as empty; its rich-text reference styling and final F01/F02 qualification language were preserved.

Native screenshots checked all four screen states, the eight-module Build sheet and updated legacy note. JSX verified main controls and source actions at 48px, next-quarter links at 44px, and recovery at 48px. Arithmetic checks reproduced the midpoint difference and both guidance ranges. Six exact affected roots were released.

Implementation proof still needs the whole mismatch → source → explicit repair → qualified calculation → evidence-close path, unresolved/denied recovery, source changes, browser Back, company changes, keyboard/focus ownership, scrolling and 200% text. Optional source/disclosure motion is limited to ≤120ms opacity/chevron transitions, absent with reduced motion. The entire reading and recovery flow must issue zero watch, alert, thesis or investigation writes.

This is a **provisional Paper assembly and source-matched build amendment**. It does not qualify final F01/F02 composition, resolve other writers’ unknown native effects, implement runtime owners or establish deployment/acceptance. Existing source and release holds remain intact.

## 12. Confluence Screener: smaller controls, explicit public-source states and a concrete producer repair

**Existing migrated file:** [Confluence Screener R2](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6Y3DCDDW541ZR38J1V52A9Y/p-2-0). The original MASTERMIND PAGES relocation reference identifies this working file. New [public-snapshot states and Build](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6Y3DCDDW541ZR38J1V52A9Y/p-3-0) and [retained filter studies](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6Y3DCDDW541ZR38J1V52A9Y/p-4-0) extend it.

### Decision: simplify the actual published list

The inspected public owner publishes at most three combinations and contains no filter implementation. Adding a filter journey would introduce hidden state and ambiguous Weekly eligibility to a very small list. The primary design now follows the actual source scope.

All eight active-looking filter bars were moved intact to clearly labeled study wrappers. Four mobile filter sheets moved with them. Their original node identities and descendants remain; they were not deleted or recreated.

| `15E-0` · Mobile dark EN | `15Z-0` | `26G-0` | `19J-0` |
| `1TI-0` · Mobile light EN | `1U3-0` | `26K-0` | `1XN-0` |

| `1A8-0` · Mobile light ZH | `1AT-0` | `26S-0` | `1ED-0` |

The eight boards now identify themselves as **Data anatomy**, preserving original 10–23 ordinals for the authority map. Counts are labeled **Published combinations / 已发布信号组合**; the unsupported today/current wording is removed from their headline. Desktop list headings state the cap and original ranking. Existing placeholder cards remain explicit anatomy references, not a proposed unavailable-input screen.

The smaller error/loading/filtered-empty reference copy now describes the actual build-time public input and labels filtering as a future study. A study must not be interpreted as an implemented client loader, manual Retry or exhaustive universe search.

### New public-snapshot states

| Root on p-3-0 | Applied state |
|---|---|
| `26Y-0` | Scope header distinguishing state fixtures from live signal data |
| `272-0` | 390px EN dark: Setup snapshot unavailable |
| `274-0` | 390px ZH light: 组合快照暂不可用 |

The unavailable state shows one explanation, a clear **Open Mastermind** destination and a short optional method disclosure. It shows no invented count, rank, trial recovery or Retry. A neutral document illustration supports the snapshot meaning without implying live signal activity.

The qualified-zero specimen shows zero published combinations and **No qualifying setups**, with the separate **Snapshot date unavailable** condition. Its meaning is limited to combinations meeting the publication rules in that snapshot; it does not claim that every signal is inactive.

The expanded 320px explanation states that the shortlist contains up to three published combinations in source order, that historical evidence explains them, and that a missing snapshot does not reveal whether any setups qualified. A new 44px action wrapper `26W-0` retains original CTA nodes; the label uses readable dark ink on its blue fill. New state buttons are 48px, with 44px method summaries.

### Exact source comparison and concrete defect

Root directly read these files at retained Macro **`338a10852dcefa5d2bd07e5c6be0955c2b1e83fd`**:

| Source | Blob | Relevant evidence |
|---|---|---|
| [scripts/build_confluence_screener.py](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/338a10852dcefa5d2bd07e5c6be0955c2b1e83fd/scripts/build_confluence_screener.py) | `118c23550f5d0bf41e39a6a3c2198e9890f534f8` | Input read/fail-soft path, shared eligible selection, top-three projection, optional defaults, public/protected output |
| [templates/confluence_screener.html.j2](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/338a10852dcefa5d2bd07e5c6be0955c2b1e83fd/templates/confluence_screener.html.j2) | `8e18461db98d7141d573cd047aa8637189ad4250` | Static public render, count/card branches, historical-method copy, generic CTA and exact-ID protected hydration |
| [tests/test_confluence_screener_page.py](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/338a10852dcefa5d2bd07e5c6be0955c2b1e83fd/tests/test_confluence_screener_page.py) | `cfc2c0ba08162f4515e62b8b0607317ea746257d` | Existing None-input, ranking/free/gated, public exclusion, protected pairing and genuine-zero-edge assertions |

The producer reads `site/factordata/tech_confluence.json`. Missing files, unreadable JSON and non-object roots become `raw=None`. Its current None context supplies empty combinations, no source date and zero counts. The template consequently renders the same **Nothing lining up today** branch as an empty eligible result. Missing `combos.long` structure also collapses toward an empty collection. **Unknown public input therefore becomes a false empty claim.**

The public page is generated HTML. Its only runtime data fetch is for separately protected ticker hydration; this inspection found no public loading, refresh, retry or filter owner. The repair belongs in the existing builder/context/template, not a new browser data lifecycle.

### Viable repair contract

1. Add a small explicit public-source availability distinction to the existing context. Validate required artifact structure before selection. Missing/unreadable/invalid required input renders unavailable; a genuinely present empty collection can produce qualified zero. Preserve existing model selection and eligibility.
2. Keep the published count `len(combos)`, eligible count `n_active_total` before the cap, and covered-company count `universe_n` distinct. Eligible combinations require active names plus supplied recent/older win rates. Zero published setups is not proof that every signal is inactive.
3. Preserve absent optional sample/edge/history fields before defaults erase missingness. Keep legitimate numeric zero and false distinct from absence. A missing edge or consistency value must not become a negative historical conclusion. Continue excluding missing required recent/older win rates under the existing selection rule.
4. Treat artifact `generated_utc` as snapshot metadata; the current `asof` is a date slice from it, not a latest market-bar clock. Validate source-date presentation and show a missing-clock message independently of otherwise usable content. Never substitute the current page-build clock.
5. Preserve supplied rank, `combo_id`, `is_free`, public match counts and original card order. One or two cards must not imply two additional published combinations. No synthetic rows are added to fill the design.
6. Retain the existing protected payload and MDXAuth controller, including its in-flight guard and exact-ID hydration. Keep the producer’s empty protected-payload overwrite when source input is missing so old paid rows are not left on disk. Public-source availability grants no identity access and proves neither lock nor entitlement.
7. The existing `cta_url` uses the Mastermind application destination through the marketing link owner. It contains no notification registration handler here. Use destination copy, not a subscription or save promise.
8. Implement the short method explanation as a native local disclosure with static text. Preserve summary focus and state across locale changes; expansion is immediate, with optional ≤120ms chevron/opacity motion and none under reduced motion. No loading shimmer for build-time public input.

The original protected-list failure specimens `23C-0` / `250-0` retain their full structure, source/access boundary and absence of manual Retry. Removing filters from the primary view required exactly three copy alignments per language: the preserved context now names the published shortlist and open guidance, rather than retired controls. No alternate protected-error state or loader was added.

If filtering is revisited for a broader future product, the retained study must define Weekly eligibility, use known supplied consistency, filter only its admitted published collection, and preserve source rank/free status. The first visible paid card must not become free. A hide/show integration would need to preserve the existing hydrator’s DOM identity; rebuilding already-hydrated cards can invalidate its current lifecycle.

### Existing tests and required proof

The current `test_build_context_none_raw` expects empty arrays/zero/no date. `test_render_html_empty_context_no_exception` checks only nonempty HTML, so it does not catch the false-empty wording. The suite’s explicit `edge_wr_test=0` case establishes a genuine zero edge; it is not missing-source or zero-combination coverage.

Extend that same suite with EN/ZH unavailable versus valid-empty copy; missing required structure versus an empty array; missing source clock versus available cards; absent optional metrics versus supplied zero/false; and one/two/three-card output. Preserve existing ranking, free/gated identity, public ticker exclusion, protected combo pairing and hydration-owner assertions.

The current protected None-input test checks the **function result**, despite its overwrite-oriented name. An isolated `render(tmp_path)` test should seed old protected data, use missing/invalid source, and read back both the unavailable public page and overwritten empty protected payload. It must prove that old identities were removed without running the miner, changing gates or altering access policy. Tests were inspected, not executed in this design pass.

### Native verification and preservation

Across all eight anatomy boards plus their retained-study destinations, final JSX found **no lost original IDs**, exactly **66 intended text changes**, and exactly the **12 planned parent moves**. Four desktop bars received only the expected layout adjustment from reparenting. All other original node styles/parents remained unchanged. Each protected failure board retained all nodes and changed only its three specified text nodes.

Native screenshots checked the four new state compositions, final Chinese and 320px wording, corrected zero specimen, representative desktop/mobile anatomy views, desktop/mobile retained controls, both protected-failure variants, authority map and Build sheet. No clipping was observed in the inspected 320px and Chinese views. Twenty-eight exact affected roots were released. The original governed-reference page was not edited.

This is applied Paper consolidation and a precise source repair/build plan. No runtime source patch, test execution, model run, auth change, merge, release or deployment was performed.


## 13. Crypto Intelligence: saved identity, historical return and safe recovery

**Applied Paper amendment:** [Saved case and historical return](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6Y3F0NXNDPAKJMJGCAD759Z/p-4-0), in the existing Crypto R4 file `01M6Y3F0NXNDPAKJMJGCAD759Z`. Eight states, a ten-module Build sheet, a 30-day saved-list fixture and three previously empty interaction-scorecard rows are now present. This is design and source diagnosis; runtime implementation remains pending.

### Source identity and observed defects

The existing [Macro PR #9580](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/pull/9580) was read at actual head `5e496c24c962e85a4f7f514dd43dc61443b95cab`: open, Draft and unmerged. Relevant files were read at that same head:

- [Browser owner](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/5e496c24c962e85a4f7f514dd43dc61443b95cab/templates/crypto_intelligence_r4.js), blob `ff46d4408d243bcebb8e6cac95167b4a6ef933c4`.
- [Companion CSS](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/5e496c24c962e85a4f7f514dd43dc61443b95cab/templates/crypto_intelligence_r4.css), blob `e44434af0ea057a8d2cb34aa2d09b82988fd785c`.
- [Existing browser tests](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/5e496c24c962e85a4f7f514dd43dc61443b95cab/tests/test_crypto_intelligence_r4_browser.py), blob `1b8dd18839de3bb125f99a31d433d44d650ef9a0`.
- [Candidate view data](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/5e496c24c962e85a4f7f514dd43dc61443b95cab/site/crypto_intelligence_r4.json), blob `024278d4143ee076f4253cd295230636818e97bd`.
- [Builder](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/5e496c24c962e85a4f7f514dd43dc61443b95cab/scripts/build_crypto_intelligence_r4.py), blob `64105d836bfe13492749425bea65a3f3749e0494`, and [existing build packet](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/5e496c24c962e85a4f7f514dd43dc61443b95cab/research/CRYPTO_INTELLIGENCE_R4_BUILD_PACKET.md), blob `b936eb0a8bf2d448f91196b3b4b1c6999550e25f`.

The candidate already owns local snapshots under `mmx.crypto.r4.scenarios.v1`, native disclosures, hash routes, the dialog, toasts and language updates. The amendment extends those owners.

Source inspection found four consequential gaps:

1. Saved-list actions open a revision without passing its saved horizon. Historical review later changes the horizon, but returning to the saved parent is not explicitly modeled.
2. A missing `memory-saved` lookup falls back to the **current** horizon and `data.as_of`. A nonexistent saved case can therefore appear to have current context.
3. `stored()` returns an empty list on access, parse or collection-shape failure. The mutation owner then uses that filtered/sliced result as a write source, which can erase unreadable or unrecognized retained content.
4. `save(s)` toggles intent according to the fresh lookup. A stale Remove action can append the case again, with newly captured evidence; a stale Save can remove an already saved case. Duplicate revisions lose only the first matching entry. The named parent return and locale rerender also need a semantic focus target because `renderDrawer()` replaces inner DOM nodes.

These are source findings, not reproduced browser failures. The historical build packet’s earlier test claims are not this session’s test evidence.

### Applied states and existing-board changes

| State | Root | Purpose and visible behavior |
|---|---|---|
| 09 · Saved 30-day case, EN | `3BC-0` | Original cutoff and condition first; original case details and evidence disclosed on demand; Historical review and a separate local Remove action. |
| 10 · Historical review | `3BE-0` | Same saved title, 30-day horizon and cutoff; “No qualified comparison yet”; “← Saved case” names the verified parent destination. |
| 11 · Saved version unavailable | `3BG-0` | No borrowed asset, horizon, date or condition. Open saved snapshots is primary; Open current outlook is an explicit separate action. |
| 12 · Original evidence at 320px | `3BI-0` | The same saved-item route with its native evidence disclosure expanded; six captured readings and their own quality/date captions. |
| 13 · Saved 30-day case, ZH | `3BK-0` | Matching hierarchy and frozen meaning. Daily-close wording uses “高于” rather than implying a crossing event. |
| 14 · Older expired 7-day case | `3BM-0` | Original condition and cutoff retained; distinct expiry date; “Source details not saved” explains a missing digest without treating the record as missing. |
| 15 · Detached historical review | `3BO-0` | Same saved context; “Open saved case” replaces the current entry when there is no verified immediate parent. |
| 16 · Saved collection unreadable | `3JY-0` | “Saved snapshots couldn’t be read. No changes were made.” A deliberate Try reading again uses the same reader; current outlook remains separate. |
| Build | `3IP-0` | Ten modules covering sources, identity, navigation, horizon, evidence, focus, operations, recovery, tests and delivery. |

Page header `3B8-0` declares historical source fixtures, not personal saved records. The original interaction board `G5-0` retains its 7-day item `H4-0`; new 30-day fixture `3KD-0` sits beside it in the same Saved snapshots owner. The 7-day item maps to state 14, and the 30-day item maps to state 09. Only the original explanatory pointer `G9-0` was changed.

The original Build authority board `QI-0` previously had an empty interaction scorecard. Rows `3KG-0`, `3KN-0` and `3KU-0` now distinguish saved-case restoration, parent return, and read/remove recovery. The UI column explicitly says the runtime amendment is pending. Only its pointer `QM-0` was changed; the public-route cutover gate `QQ-0` remains intact.

### Exact fixture meaning

The 30-day case is `r4-display:2026-10-17:30:continuation`, BTC, Upside test. Its original cutoff is **17 October 2026**, expiry **16 November 2026**, and condition is a daily close above **$86,595**, followed by stronger spot demand. The level basis is 60 prior daily closes from 18 August through 16 October, with a low of $64,681. It must not inherit the 7-day high of $86,507. The illustrative saved-at value uses the existing test anchor of 18 October 2026 at 12:00 UTC; it does not prove a user action.

The older 7-day case is `r4-display:2026-10-17:7:failure`, Downside test. It retains the original daily-close threshold below **$81,566** and expiry **24 October 2026**. Its absent `issue_evidence` digest does not erase its original condition or cutoff.

The six evidence rows come from the candidate’s captured-evidence projection:

| Reading | Captured state | Captured quality | Saved snapshot date |
|---|---|---|---|
| Price structure | Bullish | Snapshot only | 17 Oct 2026 |
| Demand & capital | Updates pending | Stale at capture | 17 Oct 2026 |
| Leverage | Low | Observed | 17 Oct 2026 |
| Holders & network | Positive | Snapshot only | 17 Oct 2026 |
| Macro conditions | Headwind | Snapshot only | 17 Oct 2026 |
| Participation | Coverage incomplete | Insufficient | 15 Oct 2026 |

Family dates are `snapshot_as_of`, not verified observation timestamps. Bitcoin’s cutoff remains 17 October; the broader-market clock remains 15 October. Source paths are internal field provenance, not publisher URLs. No current freshness, probabilities, validated performance or model allocation is inferred.

### Navigation and rendering contract

- Resolve one usable saved record by **exact revision** before render and again before an action. Validate retained authority, supported saved horizon, cutoff and original case fields. A missing digest alone is a supported legacy limitation.
- Pass validated `s.horizon_days` when opening a saved-list item. A 30-day case entered from a 7-day view remains 30-day through review and parent return; closing the managed drawer journey restores the 7-day ancestor.
- When the existing history metadata identifies an immediate saved-item parent for the **same** revision, parent return goes back one entry. A review opened from a detached saved-item can still have that valid parent; `detachedOrigin` alone does not disqualify it.
- With a usable record but no verified immediate parent, Open saved case replaces the current entry through the existing update owner. A missing or ambiguous record opens the unavailable state. An unreadable collection uses state 16, not an empty list.
- Close retains the existing whole-journey exit semantics. It is not the implementation of the parent action. Do not add another router, navigation stack, saved-case store or lifecycle.
- Capture semantic action/disclosure identities against the panel key and exact saved revision before rerender. Restore disclosures before reading position; focus the recreated Historical review control on parent return. Preserve the outer opener separately.
- Locale changes create neither a new history entry nor a storage write. Restore state only when the same saved case still resolves; otherwise focus the unavailable message. Outer Close restores only a connected, visible opener in the active view, with that view’s heading as fallback.
- The existing dialog is the scroll container, with a sticky header. Do not create a second scrolling body. The inspected native frames establish content fit at 320px/390px, not runtime scrolling. Keep native disclosure semantics, 44px parent/Close/disclosure targets, 48px primary actions and existing reduced-motion handling.

The interface keeps the condition visible and defers longer sequence, invalidation, counter-evidence, expiry and level-basis explanations to Original case details. Evidence remains a single disclosure. The seven initially reviewed screen bodies needed no added explanatory paragraph; the storage-read state addresses a distinct subsequently verified source gap.

### Explicit snapshot operations and recovery

The existing mutation function should take the **intent chosen by the user**:

| Intent and fresh state | Required behavior |
|---|---|
| Save; absent | Apply existing eligibility, then append the dated snapshot. |
| Save; already present | Already saved; preserve the original saved-at and evidence. |
| Remove; present | Remove every exact match for the revision and preserve unrelated entries. |
| Remove; already absent | Idempotent completion with no write and no evidence capture. |
| Either; unreadable collection | No write; show collection unavailable. |

A ready/unavailable result belongs inside the existing reader. Preserve the full valid collection for mutation separately from the displayed filtered/capped set. Do not silently clean up unrelated legacy or unrecognized entries during removal. Retain the existing capacity policy for an actual new save; no migration is added.

After a write, verify the intended postcondition before claiming success. Unconfirmed outcomes have a separate message and no automatic replay. A fresh local-storage read is not an atomic cross-tab transaction; implementation must not claim a compare-and-swap guarantee it does not provide.

Recovery copy distinguishes a requested version from collection access, an already absent removal, and an unconfirmed write. Missing or unreadable saved content must never be automatically replaced with current context. The same existing reader may be invoked deliberately by Try reading again; no retry service or background owner is introduced.

### Focused implementation proof still owed

Extend the existing browser tests rather than create a parallel harness. They already cover a route/viewport matrix, a 30-day deep link, original saved evidence, missing current scenario, legacy digest and short mobile evidence scroll. Additional proof must include:

- A saved 30-day case entered from a 7-day view → review → one-level parent → full Close to 7 days.
- Detached saved-item with a known parent versus truly detached review.
- Missing, malformed and duplicate revisions with no current-context fallback.
- Access, parse and collection-shape failures with zero storage writes.
- Stale Remove without re-addition; stale Save without deletion, renewal or evidence replacement; duplicate removal preserving unrelated entries.
- Failed write/readback with no false success or automatic replay.
- English/Chinese disclosure, reading position and semantic focus continuity; a locale change after removal; 320px/390px keyboard, scroll and reduced-motion behavior.

No tests were executed and no functional browser proof is claimed in this design pass. Existing sign-in/release gates were not bypassed.

### Native checks, preservation and release

All eight state compositions were inspected as native screenshots. Final Chinese and legacy copy, the 320px evidence disclosure, original interaction board, filled authority scorecard and Build sheet were checked; the focus/storage modules were also inspected individually. No clipping was observed in those views.

Final JSX preserves **all 64 original interaction-board IDs** with only `G9-0` changed, plus the two-node 30-day item. It preserves **all 37 original Build-board IDs** with only `QM-0` changed, plus the three six-node scorecard rows. Original source drawer `GB-0`, old saved item `H4-0`, expiry notice `H8-0`, alert gate `HE-0` and public-route gate `QQ-0` retain their original text, styles and parents. Scenario page `BB-0` is byte-identical in JSX.

The newer relative-comparison page `p-3-0` was not targeted. Its original basic-info fields and artboard metadata were unchanged; the only basic-info difference was the addition of this session’s new page to the file’s page list. The archive was not edited.

## 14. T47 ranked search: truthful zero matches and one recovery action

**Applied in the existing draft:** [T47 search specimens](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6Y2SGKNTEESDJX96ZSJJGPF/p-1-0) and [No-match visibility Build references](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6Y2SGKNTEESDJX96ZSJJGPF/p-2-0), file `01M6Y2SGKNTEESDJX96ZSJJGPF`.

The mobile no-match example reported **50 assets in the ranked snapshot / 0 matching**, followed by **Expand ranks 21–50**. The count covered all supplied rows, so that affordance suggested hidden matching results that did not exist. Root inspection confirmed the same contradiction in all eight EN/ZH, light/dark, desktop/mobile variants. Each also repeated Clear search inside the no-match body, in addition to the existing query-area action.

### Applied changes

All eight zero-match specimens now retain the query, exact count, one existing query-area Clear action, no-match message and source/date/provider/coverage strip. Their redundant body Clear and lower-rank disclosure were moved to labeled retained references on the Build page, preserving their IDs. No delete or archive operation was used.
