| Desktop · Light · ZH | `35-0` | `4V-0` | `55-0` | `5A-0` / `5C-0` | `10Q-0` |
| Desktop · Dark · EN | `69-0` | `7Z-0` | `89-0` | `8E-0` / `8G-0` | `10U-0` |
| Desktop · Dark · ZH | `9D-0` | `B3-0` | `BD-0` | `BI-0` / `BK-0` | `10Y-0` |
| 390 · Light · EN | `CH-0` | `E9-0` | `EL-0` | `EP-0` / `ER-0` | `112-0` |
| 390 · Light · ZH | `FO-0` | `HG-0` | `HS-0` | `HW-0` / `HY-0` | `116-0` |
| 390 · Dark · EN | `IV-0` | `KN-0` | `KZ-0` | `L3-0` / `L5-0` | `11A-0` |
| 390 · Dark · ZH | `M2-0` | `NU-0` | `O6-0` | `OA-0` / `OC-0` | `11E-0` |

One existing contract text node per variant was updated: `2U-0, 5Y-0, 92-0, C6-0, FD-0, IK-0, LR-0, OY-0`. The revised wording makes zero-match suppression explicit while preserving the original compact-match behavior: a rank-21 match opens the existing disclosure, and Clear restores the exact pre-search choice.

The existing rest, matching-result and no-emitted-row specimens were preserved. The newer paired Clear-return boards **`P9-0` and `UV-0` are byte-identical in final JSX**. Their prior work already correctly distinguishes originally closed and originally open cases; this amendment extends that contract.

Build root **`10H-0`** has five modules covering scope, supplied-row search, captured disclosure choice, focus/progressive enhancement and focused proof. Its retained-control references are builder material, not additional user interface.

### Exact transition contract

Capture the pre-search open/closed value once when the query transitions from empty to nonempty. Editing one nonempty query into another must not overwrite it. Apply the same empty/nonempty interpretation consistently with the host’s query semantics.

The required sequence is:

1. Start from either an originally collapsed or originally expanded supplied list.
2. Enter a nonempty zero-match query. Withhold the lower-rank disclosure, keep one Clear action and keep the source strip.
3. Change to a query matching rank 21. Preserve the existing compact-match specimen: the original lower-rank disclosure opens and exposes that matching row. The row remains rank 21.
4. Clear, nonempty-query Escape, or manually empty the field. Restore the original expansion choice and the original ranked list. The next search starts from the reader’s then-current choice.

This pass intentionally **does not remove the disclosure throughout all nonempty searches**. That broader alternative would alter the accepted compact-match examples and is unnecessary to fix the observed zero-result contradiction.

Keep focus in the search input during typing; do not scroll or move focus automatically when a result appears. Announce the changed count through the existing polite status. If a control being withheld owns focus, transfer focus to the named search input before or with the visibility change. Clear returns to that same input. Preserve full focus outlines and the existing targets of at least 44px.

The enhancement remains local. No provider request, reranking, persistent store, history entry or new route is added. Initialize only after the owner has its row inventory and handlers. With JavaScript unavailable or initialization failed, hide enhancement controls and preserve every original ranked row, source receipt and disclosure.

### Source fit and qualification

This is a synthetic T47 component draft, not an observed runtime search. Its **RIG approval, genuine baseline/current-source captures and runtime fit remain unqualified**, and its original R4/application-source/data-contract exclusions are preserved.

For comparison, the root-read [Crypto R4 candidate browser owner](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/5e496c24c962e85a4f7f514dd43dc61443b95cab/templates/crypto_intelligence_r4.js) has an existing `filterMarkets()` function with URL query/filter/sort state, supplied asset rows, a result count and an empty-result element. It has **no ranks-21–50 disclosure**. That source is not evidence that the T47 prototype is implemented or directly compatible. A future integration must first identify the accepted host and reuse that owner; this design amendment does not introduce a competing search implementation.

### Verification

Native screenshots checked all eight corrected no-match sections, a retained mobile-control reference, the mobile contract and the five-module Build sheet. The narrowed mobile views fit without clipping.

The JSX comparison accounted for **908 original nodes across the eight boards**, all retained across the original and Build pages. It found exactly **16 intended parent moves** and **8 contract text changes**. Four relocated desktop Clear buttons lost only their now-unneeded `flexShrink: 0` during reparenting; no other original styles changed. All zero-match sections contain exactly one Clear action and no lower-rank expansion action. Both Clear-return companions remained byte-identical.

Seventeen exact roots were released: eight amended original boards, eight retained references and Build `10H-0`. All mutations returned observed applied responses. No runtime code, automated tests, browser behavior, source data, approvals, release holds or deployment was changed or claimed.

### Frontier reassessment

A fresh native resource listing returned 81 resources, including open archived files; it is not a count of active products. The migrated families are now individually discoverable. Bounded reviews found substantial newer source/interaction work in Government Revenue R13, Bonds R12, Returns R4 and Intraday Flow R4, so those existing amendments were preserved. Shared Market Search/Comparison and the inspected illustrative long-read also already supplied the relevant context and limitations. The next phase should follow a demonstrated gap rather than duplicate these completed states.

## 16. T47: an executable transition reference with narrowly scoped proof

The corrected draft interaction now has an original, self-contained implementation reference in this programme:

- [ranked-search-state-reference.mjs](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/7dba2998498498ffcaa8609deb44759c79289809/research/design_candidates/production_design_20261102/audit_20261108/ranked-search-state-reference.mjs)
- [ranked-search-state-reference.test.mjs](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/7dba2998498498ffcaa8609deb44759c79289809/research/design_candidates/production_design_20261102/audit_20261108/ranked-search-state-reference.test.mjs)

Both files were saved and read back exactly. The module was also read at the test-file commit to verify that **`7dba2998498498ffcaa8609deb44759c79289809` contains the exact tested pair**.

This is **pure transition/projection reference code**, not a patch to an installed application or a new state service. It has no DOM operations, network calls, persistence, routes, timers or background work. The existing host remains responsible for its supplied population, disclosure element, status announcement, source availability, rendering and focus.

### API and data contract

| Function | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `createRankedSearchState({ inventoryKey, expanded })` | Initialize from the caller’s current supplied-list identity and existing disclosure choice. |
| `transitionRankedSearch({ rows, state, inventoryKey, event })` | Return the next immutable state, projection and an optional one-time Clear focus request. |
| `projectRankedSearch({ rows, state, inventoryKey })` | Derive matching and visible rows without effects or repeated focus requests. |

Rows carry stable caller-owned IDs and the host’s existing `primary`/`secondary` grouping. Symbol and name may be absent; other source fields, rank and missing values remain unchanged. The reference does not infer grouping from rank, row position or the number 20. Returned rows retain their original object identities and order.

Events are query change, Clear and disclosure choice. The pre-search open/closed value is captured once. Nonempty query edits and temporary disclosure changes do not replace it. A lower-group match temporarily opens the existing disclosure. Zero-match suppression changes visibility, not the remembered baseline. Clear and input-empty restore the same list state; only explicit Clear requests the established search-input focus target.

Matching is literal and case-insensitive within **symbol OR name**. It adds no trimming, fuzzy matching, tokenization, punctuation normalization or regular-expression interpretation. Empty input exits search; literal whitespace is not automatically treated as empty.

Invalid identities, groups, state and query types fail explicitly rather than being silently converted into zero results. A changed caller-supplied inventory key requires explicit reinitialization. The reference does not establish whether an empty supplied array means ready-empty, loading, unavailable or partial data; the host must classify that source state.

**Additional declared design decision:** a positive query matching only primary rows also withholds the empty secondary disclosure. A secondary-group match retains its existing disclosure. The original T47 matrix had no primary-only specimen; this is an explicit reference extension, not a claim about existing runtime behavior or a silent replacement of its compact-match design.

### Offline verification

The exact final files were executed locally with **Node v24.22.1**:

```sh
node --test ranked-search-state-reference.test.mjs
```

The runner reported **16 tests, 16 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped**. That count includes the two originally-open/originally-closed subcases.

The checks cover:

- Zero matches → rank-21 match → Clear from both original disclosure choices.
- Input-empty restoration versus Clear’s one-time focus request.
- Several query changes, temporary toggles, repeated query and a later search with a new resting choice.
- Literal punctuation, spaces, case-insensitive symbol/name matching and no cross-field combined match.
- Primary-only results, empty supplied inventory and missing names/values.
- Supplied object identity, nonconsecutive ranks, source order and caller-assigned grouping.
- Hidden-control stale events, explicit inventory rebinding and invalid IDs/groups/query/state.
- No mutation of frozen source rows or prior state.

This is offline reference logic proof. It does **not** prove DOM visibility, native disclosure events, screen-reader announcement, focus, scrolling, keyboard integration, no-JavaScript fallback, source readiness, application fit, CI, release or deployment. Those remain host integration checks. In particular, programmatic disclosure opening must not be mistaken for a new user baseline, and a focused control must be handled before the host hides it.

Exact file identities:

| File | Git blob | SHA-256 of the local tested UTF-8 file |
|---|---|---|
| Module | `5d184009b8359f11af6eb7121626bc63144ef037` | `8b229121710fc1fabb1b19f6b70c7563768bd0af2d22a7d4a8c6a829390561a0` |
| Tests | `45abf4b4e8db022748150c04978fd7a7ce3ab27c` | `cd1eddf151c1af4ab37ae1effc764d5920eb4801cfbfa04cf1350041ab63720d` |

### Linked back into Paper

The existing [T47 Build sheet](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6Y2SGKNTEESDJX96ZSJJGPF/p-2-0), root **`10H-0`**, now includes module **06 / `124-0`**, linking the exact reference commit, API, test command, proof and remaining integration limits.

Only two existing Build text nodes changed (`11K-0` and `120-0`), to distinguish the applied native scope from the separate reference proof. Three nodes were added for module 06. Final JSX confirmed no other original Build changes, the new module was visually checked, and only `10H-0` was reopened and released for this extension.

The eight native search specimens and the P9/UV Clear-return companions were not edited in this phase. Original R4, RIG, baseline/current-source and runtime qualifications remain. This adds executable builder evidence within the existing programme without claiming a shipped search feature.

| [templates/_all_tools_menu.html.j2](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/blob/4bd1948ac0d9ecf40301caf70cbf8cf1619fd2c4/templates/_all_tools_menu.html.j2) | `3259b4e5c6305fd2f9a33bea65141efb0528680a` | Shared, strictly enabled dialog host; presentation and behavior remain with the incumbent shared assets. |

The current source presents a category rail. **The compact chooser is a proposed presentation extension, not a claim that this source already implements it.** It must dispatch the same category action and read the same current groups. It adds no separate route catalogue, global market selector, saved preference, provider fetch, permission owner or automatic refresh.

The fetched adoption README contains historical and newer verification records. Its claimed runs were not executed by this session and were not promoted into acceptance for the new selector.

### State and focus contract

The six-module native Build root **`31M-0`** binds the proposal to that source:

1. A nonempty tool query searches every category while retaining the prior browsing group. The display-only label All categories must not overwrite that group.
2. Clear removes the query and returns to the previous group, with focus on search. Show available tools uses the existing reset to All destinations. Start here remains the current-section starting view.
3. Choosing China or another group during search uses the existing category action: clear the query, change directory group, reset the directory scroll as the owner already does. It neither changes the page’s market nor opens a destination.
4. Opening or cancelling the chooser changes no category/query. Selection closes it and returns focus to its trigger. Escape closes the chooser first and stops propagation; a later Escape dismisses All Tools normally. Locale and IME behavior retain their existing owner.
5. Preserve withdrawn destinations for the current opening, their positions and unavailable counts, render/click/auxiliary-click/context-menu checks, exact URLs and external-tab behavior. A category or locale redraw cannot revive a withdrawn link. Explicit close/reopen re-collects source navigation.
6. All choices remain reachable at 320px and enlarged text through the existing dialog scrolling arrangement. Optional disclosure opacity may settle within 120ms; reduced motion is immediate and input is never delayed.

### Native evidence and remaining proof

Before/after comparison retains **all 1,558 parsed original div IDs** across the eight amended phones and desktop Research board. There are exactly eight original-rail parent changes and the three intended text edits. Native serialization additionally materialized default `fontSize:12px` / `lineHeight:16px` on the two Research root frames; their descendant text/style records are unchanged. The root height grows by ten pixels because a 34px legacy rail is now a 44px control. No catalogue destination, description, count, badge, row order or original row style changed.

The existing scrolled Research references **`2VN-0` and `2YC-0` are byte-identical**. Native screenshots check the 390px and 320px expanded choosers, the no-match screen, the Research header, the complete Build sheet and retained controls. All category labels and selected marks fit. The completed working indicators for **thirteen exact roots** were released with an observed applied response.

No application tests or browser interactions were executed for this proposal. The existing All Tools test/browser owners still owe the combined Research → query → Clear, query → Show all, query → China, locale, source withdrawal, modified-link, Escape, close/reopen, focus and enlarged-text proof. No deployment, opt-in expansion or release acceptance was performed.


## 20. Catalyst — selected-case identity survives filtering

**Applied and visually checked, 9 November 2026.** This is the twenty-second existing Paper file improved: [Catalyst Intelligence — Decision Desk and Integration](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6Y2VTQBVC9NA39HNG1DYWFH/p-2-0). Five new interaction specimens and a seven-module Build sheet are on [Proposed · Selected case and filters](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6Y2VTQBVC9NA39HNG1DYWFH/p-3-0).

### Current-source defect

Root independently fetched Terminal **`3e2f8db782b0ba2f98343edf5199a4b8ce921ec9`**. The current component is byte-identical to the earlier comparison at `cfe14c49...`:

The component derives `watches` from local filters and then selects `watches.find(row => row.theme_id === selectedId) || watches[0] || null`. Its missing-selection notice instead checks the full `view.cases`. An explicit Defense selection followed by the Energy filter can therefore display Energy detail while the URL still names Defense, without an absence notice. Copy case link then uses the substituted detail. The whole detail also sits inside the nonempty-results branch, so a zero-match query hides a still-eligible selected case.

These are source-demonstrated paths, not an executed browser reproduction. The existing tests separately cover filtering, selection, history, basic copy and missing-case notice. The missing-case assertion checks only the notice, not the absence of substitute detail. Combined identity and stale-copy behavior remain unproved.

### Applied states and fixture identity

| Root | Specimen | Intended result |
|---|---|---|
| `10J-0` | 01 · 1440px desktop | Energy list with one result; explicitly selected Defense detail remains visible with an outside-filter notice and one Clear filters action. |
| `117-0` | 02 · 390px EN | Energy filter plus query `solar` yields zero list matches; the selected Defense detail and copy action remain visible. |
| `11D-0` | 03 · 320px ZH | Same zero-match/selected-case distinction with readable percentage-point units. |
| `11J-0` | 04 · 390px EN | Explicit case absent from a successfully resolved eligible snapshot; show an absence notice and an Energy alternative requiring Inspect evidence. No substitute detail or enabled case-copy action. |
| `11P-0` | 05 · 320px ZH | Same explicit-absence behavior in Chinese. |
| `10D-0` | Build | Seven modules bind selection, URL, copy status, authorization, fixture variants and focused proof to the current consumer. |

All new numerical examples come from the **existing test fixture**, with date **16 October 2026**. Defense is `theme_id=defense`, `rel_5d=0.0165` → **+1.65 pp**, and `pct50=0.6` → **60%**. Energy is `theme_id=energy_complex`, **+6.21 pp** from `0.0621`, and **75%** from `0.75`. They are explicitly marked as interaction/test specimens, not market evidence.

The two absence designs describe a **test-only derived variant**: remove Defense from eligible cases, keep its coverage inventory row with `watch=false`, set `watch_count=1`, and use a distinct read/generation context. They do not invent a new source observation date. This is not a claim that the real dated source had that state. The original 14 October source-bound Paper examples and their separate Defense economics were preserved.

### Builder correction

The repair remains within the existing component:

- Resolve an explicit valid `selectedId` from the full current authorized `view.cases`. Derive the filtered list independently.
- Use the first filtered row as the initial default only when no explicit selection exists.
- Keep detail outside the filtered-empty ternary, while retaining the existing successful authorized-view boundary. Zero list results do not erase an eligible explicit case.
- An explicit case absent from a successfully resolved current snapshot gets the absence notice, no alternative detail and no enabled copy action. Loading, unavailable reads and denied access must not be reclassified as absence.
- Filter, query and Clear change the list. Only explicit Inspect evidence changes selected case/URL. Browser history restores the explicit case even when current filters exclude it.
- A new authorized snapshot may update the selected theme’s date, values, summary and cautions together. Never retain an old unauthorized payload or combine generations.

Copy case link carries `tab=catalysts&case=<theme_id>`; it does **not** pin historical source generation. The current asynchronous copy status can also arrive after a different case has been selected. The Build contract therefore binds status to the copy attempt and current selection/authorized-read context, retiring obsolete success/failure after a newer attempt, selection, snapshot, loading or access change. It does not claim to cancel an issued clipboard write, retry automatically or copy a replacement case.

No second state, route, identity, fetch, cache or refresh owner is introduced. The current workspace already clears prior-account/read data and holds access denial under its identity/revision. Independent Defense evidence stays with its existing lane.

### Native verification and preservation

Native screenshots cover the desktop, both zero-result phones, both absent-case phones, the original contract after its pointer amendment, and the seven-module Build sheet. Numeric units, source dates and 44px actions fit; the Chinese pp suffix uses a smaller inline label rather than losing the unit.

Static readback confirms one Clear filters and one Copy case link in each zero-result specimen, and no copy action in either absence specimen. These are design-tree checks, not interaction tests.

Original discovery `LK-0`, phone discovery `QE-0` and independent Defense `O3-0` are **byte-identical**. The original contract `T5-0` retains all **41 parsed div nodes**. Only `U4-0` gains the current pointer **p-3-0 / 10D-0**, with the expected native `whiteSpace:pre-wrap` serialization for its new line. All prior source/access conditions remain.

Another writer created **p-4-0 / R2 · Manual review condition flow** during this phase. One attempted header addition returned **DOCUMENT_CHANGED** before applying. The original target `10J-0` was read back and contained only its original cloned header; no addition had appeared. The new page was the observed document difference. Root made a fresh, separately evaluated header addition on the same owned target/carrier after that readback. No unknown effect was replayed; p-4-0 and the other writer’s manual-review work were not touched.

The **seven exact affected roots** were released with an observed applied response. No runtime component patch, application test, browser interaction, clipboard effect, account request, merge or deployment was performed in this Paper phase.

### Required focused proof

The existing consumer/test owner should cover Select Defense → Energy filter → copy → Clear → Back/Forward; zero-match query; missing eligible case with inventory retained; no-explicit-case default; coherent same-theme snapshot replacement; loading/access clearing; copy A then choose B; two copy attempts completing in reverse order; and refresh/access change during copy. Keyboard and responsive checks must keep filter focus stable, retain explicit Inspect heading focus, and move focus from a removed detail only when its focused control actually disappears.

## 21. Catalyst executable selection reference and exact manual-copy recovery

**Applied and verified on 9 November 2026.** This continues §20 in the same existing Catalyst file and existing programme branch. It adds two native phone states and a tested, isolated builder reference; it does not patch the runtime component.

### Executable reference and exact saved evidence

The [selection reference](catalyst-selection-state-reference.mjs) and [focused tests](catalyst-selection-state-reference.test.mjs) are saved together at Macro commit **`056abf0a324c7223cf0f5338684c024a3320a5d3`**, with exact content read back from that commit.

| Artifact | Git blob | SHA-256 |
|---|---|---|
| `catalyst-selection-state-reference.mjs` · 5,606 bytes | `a5df962524b6b06f6457c99f2506c12876a5a2fe` | `4125d0c6f860cb86831d870b14e37ff4077f0033b5d2732eb4e21d5174d06c78` |
| `catalyst-selection-state-reference.test.mjs` · 10,695 bytes | `7b3f745d1d7929d8c6b937a9d9ead4ee5d2d4732` | `3bd915e6b3f372e0b28e97b347ddcf58b1719326bf4aa52e23fe310635c12539` |

Executed `node --test catalyst-selection-state-reference.test.mjs` on **Node v24.22.1**: **22 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped**, exit 0. This is actual offline execution of the saved reference/test pair.

`deriveCaseSelection` takes the existing owner's authorized eligible cases, already-filtered visible IDs, requested identity and read state. It does not sort, filter, fetch, clone rows, construct a URL or create an independent state owner. It preserves source row identity and caller ordering. The returned projection and array are frozen; caller-owned rows are not frozen, so the caller must supply immutable snapshots.

Only explicit `null` means “no requested case” and permits the first visible default. Missing, empty or malformed requested identity is rejected. Resolved inputs reject duplicate identities and visible IDs outside the authorized eligible set. Pending, unavailable, authentication and access states return before inspecting stale payloads; they cannot produce stale detail, a misleading absence state or a copy identity. The URL's requested identity remains the existing owner's separate responsibility.

An explicit eligible case survives filtering, zero matches, Clear and history restoration. An explicit absent case never falls back to another visible row. A same-theme replacement returns the new canonical row as a whole, preserving distinctions such as missing versus zero.

`isCopyCompletionCurrent` accepts only live, matching owner-supplied attempt and read-context tokens plus a coherent selected/copy identity. The owner must retire these tokens when the attempt, selected theme, account/read revision, history or component lifetime changes. Returning A → B → A cannot revive A's old status; unchanged content generation does not make a new authorized read the old context. The predicate does not create or retire tokens, cancel an issued clipboard write, retry, navigate or prove the consumer is wired correctly.

The 22 checks cover explicit selection outside and inside zero-result filters; Clear/history; missing eligible case; default order and no default; four non-resolved states; coherent snapshot replacement; invalid inputs; canonical object/value/order preservation; current copy completion; selection and account A → B → A; overlapping attempts; same-generation new reads; blocked/missing completion; uninitialized context; and disposal.

### Exact current-expression characterization

A separate offline characterization evaluated the extracted filtering, selected-row and missing-selection expressions from the §20 current component at Terminal `3e2f8db782b0ba2f98343edf5199a4b8ce921ec9`, blob `1f8cc491040ebbf11851bca2759316406a55276b`:

| Input | Derived selected row | Missing-selection result |
|---|---|---|
| Requested `defense`; Energy filter | `energy_complex` | `false` |
| Requested `defense`; Energy filter and query `solar` | `null` | `false` |

This reproduces substitution and selected-detail loss in the exact extracted expressions. It did **not** mount React, exercise browser history or write to a clipboard. The application test owner and production browser proof remain as specified in §20.

### Copy failure must expose the prepared destination

The current `catalystOpportunityLex.ts` failure text directs the user to the address bar. That is incomplete when there is no explicit `case=` parameter: filtering can choose a default case without updating the address. The current copy handler constructs a case URL from that selected row, so the manual fallback must expose that same prepared URL rather than a potentially less specific address.

| Root | New native state | Action |
|---|---|---|
| `1HL-0` | 06 · Copy failed · Exact manual link · 390 EN | Show a labeled selectable case URL and 44px **Select link** action. |
| `1I6-0` | 07 · 复制失败 · 精确关注项链接 · 320 中文 | Same behavior with **选中链接** and readable wrapping. |
| `1JJ-0` | Build module 08 | Code identity, executed result, API boundary and current-expression characterization. |
| `1JN-0` | Build module 09 | Exact prepared-link recovery and the remaining host proof. |

Both screens use the explicitly illustrative test URL `https://example.test/discover?tab=catalysts&case=defense`. This URL must never ship. The real owner must retain the same captured `url.toString()` for the eligible attempt, preserving the current origin, other query parameters and hash. **Select link** only focuses/selects the text; it is neither a new clipboard write nor navigation. **Copy case link** may retry only through a new explicit user action.

Retire the displayed prepared URL and success/failure status together on selected-theme changes, including a default changed by filtering, as well as account/read/history changes and disposal. A late callback must not restore either. Existing Build module 04 now states this; module 07 distinguishes the newly executed offline proof from React/browser checks still owed.

### Native readback and limits

Native screenshots were inspected for both phones and the new copy-recovery Build module. Full URLs wrap without truncation; labels, failure explanation and 44px actions fit. Exact JSX readback confirms both URLs, all nine Build module identities and the updated lifecycle/proof wording. The existing fixture/source qualification remains visible. These are native layout and text checks, not interactive clipboard tests.

The exact three roots `10D-0`, `1HL-0`, `1I6-0` were finished with an observed applied response. Catalyst now has **seven new selection/recovery specimens, a nine-module Build sheet and nine unique affected roots** across §§20–21. The original source-bound discovery, separate Defense lane and concurrent p-4-0 manual-review work remain outside this phase's mutation set.

Manual text selection, mobile context menus, actual URL construction, clipboard permission/pending/failure, keyboard focus and stale-completion handling in the existing React consumer remain unverified here. No runtime patch, account request, merge, deployment or production acceptance was claimed.

## 23. Catalyst — implemented selection/copy correction and actual consumer proof

### Bounded source ownership

The source mismatch documented in §§20–21 was still present after the original Catalyst PR1038 merged. The follow-up refreshed the affected Terminal source to protected **929e008f689bc834662f52f845d070db3fc8eb00**; the relevant component, workspace, decoder, fixture, lexicon and test preimages were unchanged from the prior inspected source. The original programme's entitlement, adverse-case, scientific and production obligations were preserved rather than claimed by this UI repair.

A new operation-bound Terminal workspace was acquired through the installed owner: **paper-catalyst-selection-copy-20261109**, branch **sol/web-paper-catalyst-selection-copy-20261109**. A first acquisition returned NOT_APPLIED/REPOSITORY_SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE. Read-only diagnosis established the canonical source binding, present base object and absence of a registered operation workspace; a bounded requalified acquisition on that same operation and carrier succeeded. The primary checkout, original PR1038 branches and other writers were not modified.

The installed Next guide, current local instructions, existing workspace/read owner and protected delivery controls were inspected. Three read-only advisors remained judgment contributors; the principal implemented the bounded diff directly.

### Corrected behavior

| Situation | Actual component behavior in the follow-up |
| --- | --- |
| Explicit eligible Defense case, then Energy filter | The list changes to Energy; the selected Defense detail and copy target remain Defense, with a short outside-filter notice. |
| Explicit eligible case, then zero matching rows | The empty list and its one Clear action remain alongside the selected detail. Clear restores the list without changing the explicit case. |
| Explicit case absent from the current eligible snapshot | A missing-case notice appears; no unrelated detail or copy action substitutes for it. |
| No valid explicit selection | Defaulting to the first visible watch waits for URL synchronization. A genuinely empty filtered list has no implicit detail. |
| Delayed copy completion | Only the current attempt may publish success/failure, and only within its current case/read context. |
| Clipboard rejection or unavailable clipboard API | The labelled read-only field contains the exact URL prepared for that attempt, including the actual selected case. Select link focuses and selects that same value without another clipboard write or navigation. |
| History, selection, new read/account, ineligible read or unmount | Old feedback and fallback URLs are retired; returning to an earlier case does not revive them. |
| Explicit Inspect, superseding navigation, or focused-detail removal | Focus follows the current explicit action, cannot be redirected by an obsolete animation-frame callback, and recovers to a retained notice/heading if the focused detail disappears. Typing in the filter retains focus. |

The workspace passes its existing **current result object** as an opaque read context. This adds no lifecycle, identity, query, cache, account store, retry queue, entitlement decision or API. The independent Defense panel remains outside the market list/detail readiness guard. Source values, source dates, coverage distinctions, source disclosures and destinations retain their current owners.

The clipboard guard suppresses stale feedback; an already issued operating-system clipboard write cannot be cancelled. The URL parser's existing validity rule is preserved.

### Exact source and executable verification

[Terminal PR1354](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/pull/1354) carries commit **45472fcedad31292d660d54731d4da472ca04f1e**. The typed commit and push each returned APPLIED; publisher readback and GitHub readback agree on that head.

The six source/test files are the Catalyst view, its workspace, its CSS module, its lexicon, the existing React test suite and the existing browser spec. The commit also contains an implementation explanation, original verification output, a six-blob/seven-image manifest and seven unmodified browser captures under [docs/verification/catalyst-selection-copy-20261109](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/mastermind-terminal/tree/45472fcedad31292d660d54731d4da472ca04f1e/docs/verification/catalyst-selection-copy-20261109).

| Executed check | Observed result |
| --- | --- |
| Existing React suite with new selection/copy falsifiers | **46 passed**, 0 failed; Vitest 2.1.9; process exit 0 |
| TypeScript over the complete six-file implementation/test diff | `npx tsc --noEmit`, exit 0 |
| New targeted browser journeys using the existing Playwright configuration | **18 passed**, 0 failed, no retries; process exit 0 |
| Exact source readback and diff whitespace check | Source text matches the submitted implementation; whitespace check exit 0 |
| Native visual review | Seven captures inspected: desktop EN zero/fallback, tablet ZH zero/fallback, mobile ZH zero/fallback and mobile EN fallback |

Browser coverage used desktop 1440×900, tablet 820×1180, mobile EN 390×844 and mobile ZH 320×780. The assertions exercise the real changed components with synthetic route fixtures: retained selection through zero results/Clear, missing-case suppression, exact manual URL, text selection, focus, minimum 44px copy/select targets and no horizontal overflow. Component tests additionally cover same-case overlapping attempts, late success and rejection, A→B→A and same-case history, unchanged source dates on a new accepted read, the actual workspace's account/refresh transitions, read ineligibility, unmount and focused-detail removal.

These actual-component and browser checks are distinct from the earlier **22 pure-reference checks**. They are also distinct from PR1038's historical denied test action on old tree **62c78e3fd498ed5c8c2896f4912e084f95420362**, which was not replayed or represented as completed.

### Delivery state at this milestone

A current denial exposes only the captured URL in the existing manual fallback. ### User workflow and implementation

At ≤600px the candidate enhances the existing category region with one disclosure button and a vertical list of the same source-driven buttons. Older generated pilot hosts still mount; no mandatory new host selector or static inventory is introduced. Desktop keeps its existing presentation.

The label follows actual result scope: contextual **Start here**, an applied source category, **All categories** during normalized global search, or deliberate **All destinations**. Global query preserves the previous browsing group; Clear returns to it. Category selection clears the query, changes the local directory and focuses the collapsed button. Clear and empty-state recovery focus search; the mobile full-directory footer focuses the visible category button so focus does not remain below the newly reset scroll position.

The mobile dialog is the sole scrollport. Header, category choices and destination rows share its height; category text wraps and controls have a 44px minimum. The small chevron transitions for 120ms and has no reduced-motion transition. Source-provided blueprint SVGs and the existing token system remain intact.

The existing native dialog retains focus containment. A first Escape closes only the chooser; another fresh press can close All Tools. A per-press owner prevents held/repeated Escape from consuming both levels and preserves native IME cancellation through composition-end/input/cancel order. Locale repaint defers unfinished composition, then retains stable group identity and eligible focus. Breakpoint changes normalize the outgoing scroll offset and repair only newly hidden control focus; other focused content retains focus and requests nearest visibility. Explicit toggle collapse also repairs a focused category when pointer activation did not first focus the toggle.

Source collection, exact destinations, target/new-tab semantics and the per-opening withdrawn map remain incumbent-owned. Category/locale redraw cannot revive a withdrawn href. No catalogue, router, storage, market preference, request, native dialog, focus trap, refresh/retry or publication owner was added.

## 31. GMI keyboard selection, filtered-index clarity and narrow navigation — 9 November 2026

### Applied implementation and existing owners

The original GMI [Draft PR10676](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/Macro/pull/10676) now contains commit **386937ff4b3b689be5bd4a42a1b6e17a7f3dc6eb**, parent **696f370a3b47c2abc893a7c111b5669cce66d654**, tree **ff4bf497f0948fa9f4cd01f05fc5febf8d9b38ff**. Ten published files were read back exactly. It remains Draft with the original model, admission and release holds; no Ready, relabel, auto-merge, enqueue, merge or deployment action occurred.

The new work closes source-proven workflow gaps:

- **Tree leaves work from the keyboard.** Arrow-key focus can now activate an eligible theme leaf with plain Enter or Space. The existing link and selection owner still update the URL, answer, copy context and heading focus. Parent expansion, modifiers/IME and native nested-anchor activation are preserved; repeats cannot select again or reinstate Space scrolling; empty categories have no fabricated action.
- **Search shortcuts remain available only for matching categories.** Hidden, detached or unowned targets cannot overwrite the query/lens/return fragment. A queued hidden observer entry cannot clear a valid current category. Current-link state comes from the existing ARIA attribute rather than a second cached identity.
- **Focus survives filtering and history.** Before this owner hides an active result, category or shortcut, or restores a previously collapsed group, it moves focus to the existing search input. This covers a hidden row inside a still-visible category. Surviving and unrelated focus remain unchanged.
- **Counts describe the visible results.** HBM previously showed two rows under a category count of 64. The same filtering pass now supplies one compact EN/ZH result status, category counts and subgroup counts. The two HBM matches yield two at the category level and one in each visible subgroup. Zero uses the existing no-match sentence. Clear restores generated totals; the footer says Full index / 完整索引. The static core/extended breakdown is hidden during search because tier and held/admitted review status are different dimensions.
- **Small screens retain every destination.** The full generated main content exposed 351 px of width in a 320 px English viewport. Existing primary-navigation labels now wrap with 44 px targets. Category shortcuts increase from 40 to 44 px; mobile sticky-header and destination offsets increase together to 61 px. No overflow clipping or smaller type was used.

No new selection, filter, URL, observer, data-admission, retry or persistence owner was introduced. The complete answer-first controller—including the accepted copy and locale owners—remains byte-identical to the parent.

## 38. GMI: explicit mobile category choice, preserved context and current build guidance

### A distant category is now one native choice away

The original [Draft PR10676](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/Macro/pull/10676) advances from `20179e319a3f5219488fda4f1642a49e77ed8b13` to [**9bdc486c6b7622d9f306d29e9d12d572ed88415c**](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/Macro/commit/9bdc486c6b7622d9f306d29e9d12d572ed88415c), tree **477514cccf090a03a1f9742bacb8f70167dcdadb**. All ten intended files read back exactly, the parent compare-and-swap succeeded, and the PR remains Draft/unmerged with its original holds.

At the existing ≤640 px breakpoint, a native **Category / 类别** select and separate **Go / 前往** replace the horizontally concealed chip choices. The select is a pending destination, not a claim about the category currently in view. Typing/choosing causes no navigation. Go revalidates and invokes the original anchor owner, opens its group and scrolls through the established 61 px offset. Selection remains for repeat jumps.

This two-step design follows actual keyboard observation. Disposable Chrome select probes produced intermediate change events during typeahead: eleven with immediate reset and two with retained choice while typing Category 10. An onchange jump would therefore move readers while they were still choosing. The product's real EN typeahead is now tested without scrolling or history effects; native Go click/Enter/Space each delegates once. OS popup arrow handling remains unproven and is not claimed.

The select and Go retain 44 px heights, Go is 44 px wide, and an 8 px gap fits both languages at 320 px within the existing 61 px strip. The native arrow is free of the old track fade. No explanatory paragraph is added to the product.

### Feature viability and state ownership

The existing filtered groups and retained anchors supply available IDs, order and counts. Existing language spans supply labels. Available unfiltered zero-count categories remain destinations; filtered-out categories disappear. Valid pending IDs survive locale/count changes. When a chosen category disappears but others remain, focus moves from owned Go to the select before the button is disabled and the prompt restored. When all groups disappear, the existing index hide owner moves owned focus to search.

Go rejects hidden, detached or reparented links and groups at activation. It cannot silently select the first remaining category or fall through to a native fragment. Query, lens, open=all, qualified return, unrelated hash keys and history remain with their current owners. Passive intersection updates only the existing anchor current-state marker. Equivalent filter/locale refreshes retain option-node identity.

Read-only reasoning review identified two concrete responsive defects before acceptance: focus transfer could change the intended destination when shrinking from desktop, and default focus could scroll a reader back to the desktop rail after a jump. The implemented handoff now carries an owned focused link's ID into the pending select, reveals the incoming presentation before transferring focus with preventScroll, then hides the old one. An entirely stale destination inventory recovers to existing search. Unrelated focus is preserved.

The enhancement initializes independently of IntersectionObserver. Its authored wrapper starts hidden; with scripts actually disabled, the original ten category links and 371 topic links remain useful, and native details/anchor behavior works. No new query, URL, storage, locale, observation, scroll or publication owner was introduced.

## 41 · GMI coverage separates companies, listings, core themes and clocks

The next bounded audit found a semantic defect beneath the former coverage legend. The existing producer deduplicates resolved issuer IDs but adds each unmatched security to the same identity-reason bucket. The interface presented that mixed bucket as companies. The global “any theme” hero also implied a globally unique population from summed market counts, while its theme summary mixed core and extended scopes. The footer called model generation a review-ledger date. These were corrected in the existing frontend without changing the producer, accepted model, identity/admission decisions or public projection.

### Applied source and accounting

The original [Draft PR10676](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/Macro/pull/10676) advances from **ed2a0d178b459a8bdb1cc233f257ee82d946f71a** to **e0d7550087c564529270c12049ae62782d5b0101**, tree **52a6ac8392df0d7543712bb60b427a466b4853c7**, through the same GitHub authoring carrier with an exact-head compare-and-swap. All **ten changed files** read back exactly. Draft/unmerged status and the original release-hold section were read back unchanged.

The compact **Coverage & methodology / 覆盖与方法** header retains one H1, the existing snapshot, stale-state owner, navigation, qualified return and Brain reservation. Separate US and China cards now display admitted companies, company-count bounds, matched companies and unmatched listings. Reasons are a native disclosure with explicit company units; method and source detail remains available in a second native disclosure.

| Accepted input | Displayed company population | Separate unresolved listings |
|---|---|---:|
| US | 1 admitted; 1,927 matched; bounds 1,928–1,931. Reasons: 722 identity-unconfirmed + 1,200 vendor-only + 4 without activity = **1,926 companies**. | **4** |
| China | 0 admitted and 0 matched; bounds 1–1,019. No resolved-company reason population or complete-coverage assertion. | **1,019** |

The template subtracts unresolved listings only from the mixed `IDENTITY_NOT_QUALIFIED` reason. It does not clamp mismatches, manufacture percentages or claim the two market populations are globally unique. Matching a company record and passing identity checks remain distinct. Company reasons must sum to matched companies minus the admitted numerator.

The 68-cell theme strip and its two totals now use acceptance-tier themes only: **1 core theme with admitted activity, 67 without**. The **303 extended** themes stay separate and excluded. A proposed-only note is a subset without admitted activity, not every theme containing a proposal. Theme counts measure themes and need not reconcile to distinct market-company counts.

**Snapshot as of** means `model.asof`; **Coverage model generated** means `provenance.built_at_utc`, retaining exact datetime and UTC. Source types remain undated. The accepted model uses 3 Nov 2026 as-of and was built on 4 Nov at 02:29:26 UTC. The interface does not infer a source review event from model creation or assume an overridable as-of equals the symbol-directory snapshot.

### Reconciled design and architecture

Current qualification requires a parseable timezone-bearing source `generated_utc`, valid coverage and required artifact structure. Missing, date-only or invalid source time means unavailable with unknown counts. The older Paper instruction allowing proven zero with a missing source date is therefore replaced by the current qualification contract. Removing only the clock check would leave public/protected receipt and consumer comparisons inconsistent.

[Public states & Build](https://app.paper.design/file/01M6Y3DCDDW541ZR38J1V52A9Y/p-3-0) now makes this distinction concrete:

- **278-0** remains a qualified-zero specimen, renamed **38 · Qualified zero · Test fixture · EN**. Its source clock is **2026-07-19T04:00:00Z**, taken from the current test fixture and its available-empty case. It is not a live snapshot claim.
- The count label is **ELIGIBLE COMBINATIONS**, heading **No eligible combinations**, and concise body **No active combination in this source has readings for both periods.**
- The source-built clock and absent market-observation time remain distinct. The existing **Open Mastermind** action and method disclosure keep their original geometry and navigation meaning.
- The nine-module R3 builder map now points to the actual candidate, distinguishes eligible totals from displayed cards, preserves required versus optional evidence rules and states the real action effects.
- All **171 Build** and **20 zero-state** div identities/parents remain exact. Build styles are exact. The sole zero-state style delta is Paper's `whiteSpace: pre-wrap` normalization on the explicitly two-line clock node **29Z-0**.
- Newer **R4 child 2I3-0** remains byte-exact: **39,643 characters /135 div nodes**. Its earlier source characterization is clearly distinguished from current candidate implementation.

Four guarded operations—initial text amendment, fixture rename, two-node precision refinement and finish—returned **APPLIED_RESPONSE_OBSERVED**. [PAPER_CONFLUENCE_CURRENT_CONTRACT_20261109.json](PAPER_CONFLUENCE_CURRENT_CONTRACT_20261109.json) contains full before/after JSX, exact deltas and operation results.

### Remaining concrete source work

The current source comparison identifies three bounded follow-ups:

1. **Cached old-consumer compatibility.** The candidate's complete new reader checks source hash/time, IDs, counts and unique tickers, but the producer still emits `tier_payload.v1`. An older cached page accepts that schema and joins by ordinal combination ID while ignoring the new receipt. A new artifact with reused IDs can therefore attach its identities to an older public recipe. Use one paired protocol discriminator rejected by the frozen old reader; keep the existing endpoint, auth controller and full-artifact hash. A second recipe-key lifecycle is unnecessary.
2. **Actual protected overwrite proof.** The producer writes an empty protected payload after a successful unavailable public render, but the overwrite-named test calls only `build_premium_payload(None)`. A seeded prior protected file and actual `render(tmp_path)` must prove the on-disk outcome. Do not claim overwrite after an earlier HTML-render/write failure.
3. **Honest application action.** The valid-empty source link still says “Get notified when setups fire,” but `cta_url` is the existing application destination. Paper's **Open Mastermind / 打开 Mastermind** remains the intended accurate label. The unavailable action's `location.reload()` is a page reload, not a source-generation service.

Required malformed rows currently reject the whole artifact. Do not silently drop them and alter rank/free identity. Required fire/month samples and optional edge/history/consistency fields remain different cases.

### Custody and proof limits

The [scoped adoption receipt](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/macro/pull/10705#issuecomment-6724281889) belongs to the incumbent delivery root. Its former Draft hold was released only for that bounded source delivery; current PR10705 remains Ready/unmerged with its existing admission/merge-blocked records. This phase changed no source, label, admission, runtime, entitlement or release control.

Historical **138 tests /39 browser cases /24 captures** remain tied to their older source. They were read as evidence, not rerun or recertified. The current integration has a separate semantic-proof refusal; it does not prove a Confluence implementation failure or release permission.

## 62. Crypto R4 — explicit snapshot intent, confirmed effects and truthful saved-state recovery

### Delivered source and Paper outcome

The original saved-snapshot owner could reinterpret a visible Remove as a Save after another tab changed storage, and could discard unrelated records while removing through its filtered/sliced display array. Unreadable collections appeared empty. Several unconfirmed writes announced removal. The existing `memory-saved` lookup could borrow current context when the requested saved version was missing.

These paths are repaired in dependent Draft [Macro PR #10901](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/Macro/pull/10901), head `2c04fc37d239f4bf27b6efaed98b229256ea337e`, tree `c47e1d02373379b75258241c86bf3745b80fb28b`, on `design/crypto-snapshot-safety-20261109`. Its base is the existing R4 branch at PR #9580 head `5e496c24c962e85a4f7f514dd43dc61443b95cab`. PR #9580 remains an open Draft. The incumbent branch and its original host workspace were not modified.

Paper file `01M6Y3F0NXNDPAKJMJGCAD759Z` now records the implemented storage scope and exact source pin in Build `3IP-0`. Eight Build texts were updated. State 16 / `3JY-0` received one copy correction: the general unreadable-list message no longer claims that no change occurred. All **48 existing nodes retain IDs, parents, order and styles** across the two owned roots; both were finished and read back. The state-16 specimen and three changed Build text areas were visually inspected. No product frame was replaced.

### Runtime behavior

- Save/Remove intent is captured from the displayed control. A late click does not become its opposite after another tab writes.
- Already-present Save and already-absent Remove make no write. The former preserves the original save time and evidence digest; the latter never recaptures or appends.
- Remove deletes all exact revision matches from the full readable array, including duplicates beyond the display window, while retaining unrelated parsed entries and their order.
- Only an actual new Save applies the existing fifty-recognized-snapshot retention policy. Opaque entries are preserved.
- The existing reader distinguishes ready empty data, unsupported retained entries, an unusable exact requested version and an unavailable collection. Both saved-item and memory-saved require one usable exact version with original fields, real dates and supported saved horizon. Legacy digest absence remains supported.
- A single write is followed by exact serialized readback. Throw-before-write, write-then-throw, dropped write, readback failure and mismatched readback remain unconfirmed, without success, redirect or automatic replay.
- Storage confirmation is separate from subsequent UI reads. A confirmed removal may be followed by an unreadable list; the completion remains confirmed and the list message stays neutral.
- A newer route/language/focus owner wins. Programmatic activation does not displace unrelated focus. A focus callback opening another drawer cannot be overridden by late removal focus.

This is a read/write/readback confirmation contract, **not atomic cross-tab compare-and-swap**. The tested sequential stale-intent paths do not establish simultaneous cross-tab linearizability.

### Exact inputs and preservation

The owned verification root is `/Volumes/Mastermind/agent-workspaces/verify/crypto-snapshot-20261109-0cfg9psm`. Twenty-one initial inputs were fetched at the incumbent SHA, verified against Git blobs and copied to a retained exact parent snapshot before testing. Sixteen unchanged original inputs were byte-checked after the repair. Twenty further exact-parent dependency files were restored only to resolve three missing imports in existing Python tests; no credentials or live data were copied.

Ten recorded JS transformations reverse exactly to the original source. Template and served JS are identical at blob `e0dd8e3cd11368dc666c48e3f9c1f74e0d15db96`. The builder's actual three-source version calculation changes `be2ec2a278c4` to `3786626cb4d5`. Each saved candidate HTML changes only its two CSS/JS query references. The HTML template, CSS, source JSON, builder, model projection and shared assets remain exact.

## 70. Crypto quotes — valid availability, bounded precision and isolated rounding (2026-11-09)

**Implemented, verified and saved; continuing into fallback identity clarity.** Dependent Draft [PR #10901](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/Macro/pull/10901) advances from `af9322f8e870be16f6f29fc38b8b573267347bd6` to **`0c895f7bf5585f152f7bc0c6fc5eb23b6ec057c6`**, tree `0003c00fe00d26655a19dea7af1e37b3635ffe6f`, assets `4165f78946a7`.

### Product and architecture

The existing projection now accepts asset quotes only when finite and strictly positive. An invalid price becomes null without discarding the asset, date, relative state/value, structure, risk or eligibility. Markets and the asset inspector render **Unavailable / 暂无** explicitly.

Positive quotes below 0.00000001 display **<$0.00000001**. Other sub-cent positives use up to 8 decimals with trailing zeros removed, including positive half-up boundaries. The normal money formatter is unchanged for ordinary asset prices and all Bitcoin/scenario/saved-case uses. Genuine zero and negative percentage changes retain their existing meaning.

The existing server builder adds asset-only formatting with `Decimal(str(number))`, fixed decimal output and a fresh local `Context(prec=28, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)`. The matching asset-only JavaScript formatter uses existing Intl behavior. Only the Markets price cell and inspector Price consumer adopt the new formatter. No new source fetch, model, controller, storage key, router, identity registry or comparison calculation owner is introduced.

Sub-cent parity is verified; universal currency-format parity is not claimed. The retained ordinary branch may show $1.00 server-side and $1 client-side. This is display precision after numeric parsing, not an inferred market tick size or source-quality guarantee.

### Meaningful failure and passing evidence

- Parent quote baseline, PID 19998: **18 failed / 19 passed / 201 deselected**. It reproduces zero/negative projection leakage, false $0.00 and missing-price ambiguity. Four browser contexts/pages, zero errors or external/WebSocket traffic.
- Initial candidate, PID 28668: **108 passed** (100 contract + 8 browser),15.17s pytest / 15.792s subprocess. Eight contexts/pages, zero errors or external/WebSocket traffic. Four new quote cases include 18 asset Inspect→Close journeys; existing relative-integrity paths retain their scope.
- Focused ambient-context reproduction, PID 51139: **2 failed**. A precision 2 caller raises InvalidOperation; normal precision sets caller Inexact/Rounded flags.
- Final isolated-context builder, PID 54977: **17 passed**, 1.28s pytest / 1.61s subprocess. This rechecks 15 server-price boundary cases and 2 caller-context cases. Frontend files and browser inputs remain unchanged by that refinement.

JUnit union: **110 unique passing cases = 102 contract + 8 browser**, with 15 overlapping cases between the 108-pass and 17-pass runs. This is not a complete browser-suite rerun.

Six final native captures were inspected: invalid/below-limit quotes at EN390 dark/fine-pointer and ZH320 light/touch, and the existing unavailable/history relative states. The bound fits at 320px; independent data and controls remain legible. A synthetic source fixture is explicitly distinct from actual market observations.

Publication readback, PID 58867, exit0: **nine changed source/served/test paths, 12 unchanged inputs**. Product and test transformations reverse to the exact parent. Generic money is conserved; retained JSON/CSS stay exact; both candidate pages change only their two asset-version references. **29 text/source/evidence files and six PNG binaries** match source commit readback.

## 74. Crypto native navigation — matching link context and continuous focus ownership (2026-11-09)

**Implemented, verified and saved; continuing into expired current-card qualification.** Dependent Draft [PR #10901](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/Macro/pull/10901) advances from `059b9a6acd6d84726e20781b52a66e0c2a5ec651` to **`e70fc60183c83565497c431c433b0594423cc7cb`**, tree `b45408a8854c150b6b891b8a96f3f1ced992f733`, assets `f15feec04ead`.

### Product and architecture

Native hrefs now preserve the same horizon, chart window, Markets search/filter/sort and extra parameters as ordinary route activation. The existing apply loop copies normalized parameters, removes only panel and uses the existing address serializer. Current path/query remain intact; copying or opening a link does not change the origin's state.

The existing route-click branch now respects consumed, modified and nonprimary actions. For an owned action whose outgoing view contains focus, it focuses the existing destination tab before writing history or hiding the view. It continues only if URL, locale, history signature/length and intended visible focus still belong to that action. Newer navigation, language, history or focus choices win; a removed target cancels stale continuation.

Persistent navigation keeps its focus. No new router, history primitive, focus registry, listener owner, storage write or data/model calculation. This is a bounded owned-link repair, not a claim of general focus restoration for arbitrary hash/Back/Forward transitions.

## 77. Crypto current-case review — exact return and readable entry (2026-11-09)

### Implemented outcome and source ownership

[Dependent Crypto PR #10901](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/Macro/pull/10901) now records source `0ec213ab7bb570a0cebc28f2e4112cde14ff59c2`, tree `25ecc42bba02e35bc94041a6eb0a23c1d18abd86`, assets `2698fd4fd4ed`. Parent source is `bcf8e84957017181dc323ff3c3a9f4b32b218799`. It remains Draft, open, unmerged and unarmed against incumbent PR #9580.

Valid current-case Historical review now exposes **← Scenario details / ← 情景详情** for a recognized immediate same-case/horizon parent, or **Open scenario details / 打开情景详情** for a detached or wrong-horizon entry. The context kicker is **Scenario / 情景**. Parent return restores the matching case’s disclosure, scroll and review-button focus. Close and Escape still exit the complete managed drawer journey and restore the outer context.

A newly entered current review begins at the top with the case identity and “No qualified comparison yet” visible. The final rule distinguishes a changed URL/horizon, changed serialized history state, and native incoming entries even when URL and state serialize identically. Same-entry locale refresh retains deliberate review scroll. Generic Historical review and unresolved cases do not acquire a fabricated case parent.

Current-case return remains available when the case is expired or saved storage cannot be read. Save eligibility and the storage warning/retry are separate. Native evidence Back also retains the current reader’s disclosure and semantic action focus. No historical results, freshness claims or additional data observations are created.

The existing ownership stays cohesive:

- `readingScope()` derives the case from the existing route and active-scenario resolver, requiring exact panel, nonblank supplied revision, valid cutoff and matching numeric horizon.
- `history.state.reading` remains the only persisted reading owner. Its panel/revision/horizon/cutoff tuple qualifies disclosure, scroll and focus.
- `hasDrawerParent()` accepts an optional normalized current horizon; missing `h` correctly means 7D.
- The existing single pending saved-return intent becomes `pendingCaseReturn`, carrying the expected tuple and child/parent addresses. Duplicate activation does not enqueue another Back.
- `apply()` keeps a local witness before clearing pending state and compares the complete identity after rendering.
- Current controls gain semantic keys for conditions, review, explicit Save versus Remove, storage retry and demand evidence.
- Disabled, detached, hidden, inert or unrendered restore targets fall back to the heading. No DOM/history/scroll write follows the final focus callback.

Confirmed current-case Save/Remove now preserves freshly observed open conditions and scroll, then focuses the usable resulting action. PR10901 remains Draft/open/unmerged/unarmed. The source README now has **15 workflow → contract → proof entries**, and its stale next-phase wording is corrected. The focused [current mutation contract](https://github.com/mastermindx-market-intelligence/Macro/blob/f2cb62ef2e19abbef7ebe218030c110276953912/research/design_candidates/crypto_snapshot_safety_20261109/CURRENT_SAVE_READING.md) explains the outcome, failure table, existing owners, completion sequence, tests and reproduction limits.

### Implementation through the existing owners

The parent confirmed the storage action correctly, then rebuilt the drawer directly. That closed the disclosure and lost scroll; automatic reading capture could preserve the reset. The repair separates fresh capture, ownership, eligibility and optional persistence within the existing history-state owner.

- `captureDrawerReading()` distinguishes obsolete ownership from an owned but unqualified reading. The latter can reconcile confirmed controls without reusing or clearing old metadata.
- The capture witnesses URL, language, body subtree, history length and exact metadata signature. Current identity still requires panel/revision/horizon/cutoff.
- The original full-collection storage, explicit intent, one write/readback, opaque-entry retention and existing capacity logic are unchanged. Rendering consumes the confirmed collection without another read.
- Automatic capture is suspended across replacement and projection. Explicit completion capture still works inside an enclosing render's focus callback.
- Persistence merges only `reading`, preserves other metadata and adopts `renderedHistory` only after exact intended readback. Unchanged optional failure can leave the fresh visible reading usable; it is not reported as persisted. A newer same-address metadata write or identical-metadata pushed entry ends the older continuation.
- Shared matching, disclosure/scroll projection and usable-focus qualification serve both `apply()` and `save()`. The mutation's final focus is terminal; newer callback destinations receive no late overwrite.
- Frozen saved-item removal retains its existing return to Saved snapshots. Current review-parent, whole-journey Close, detached cases and first-reading behavior remain covered.

The reasoned architecture check found no additional concrete scope necessary. It was advisory, not an invented independent-review or release gate. Root authored and verified the candidate.

|---|---|---|
